### [1] [Beyond the Hivemind: Escaping LLM Homogeneity via Meta-Persona Anchoring and Sequential Temperature Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02618)
*Tairan Fu,Javier Conde,Carlos Arriaga,Gonzalo Martínez,Pedro Reviriego,Javier Coronado-Blázquez*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Meta-Persona Anchoring与Filtered Temperature Scaling（FTS）相结合的两阶段框架，将LLM输出的语义相似度从约0.85降至0.65，缓解人工蜂群思维导致的模式坍缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM存在人工蜂群思维效应，即使高温采样下输出仍高度同质化（语义相似度约0.80-0.90），严重限制AI多样性，需有效缓解机制。

Method: 两阶段生成：先让模型自选独特人格锚定起始点；再采用双阶段采样筛，先用Top-p过滤保证语法正确，再对幸存候选施加极高温度（T≥4.0）扩展概率分布探索。

Result: 在INFINITY-CHAT数据集及~20B开源模型上，平均成对余弦相似度从0.85降至0.65，多数问题低于0.7阈值，显著减少语义收敛。

Conclusion: 该方案有效缩小人工模式坍缩与人类类型多样性差距，并开源实现以促进更多样、更具创造性的AI部署。

Abstract: Recent studies have identified an ``Artificial Hivemind'' effect in Large Language Models (LLMs) causing models to converge on a narrow, homogenized consensus even for open questions. This semantic collapse limits the diversity of AI, resulting in high inter-response similarity ($\approx 0.80-0.90$) even under high-temperature sampling. In this paper, we propose a novel mitigation framework to increase diversity: Meta-Persona Anchoring combined with Filtered Temperature Scaling (FTS). Our approach utilizes a two-stage generation process: first, the model is prompted to self-select a unique, idiosyncratic persona to anchor its starting point; second, we apply a dual-stage sampling sieve, utilizing Top-$p$ filtering to preserve grammatical validity followed by extreme temperature scaling ($T \ge 4.0$) on the surviving candidates to explore the broadened probability distribution. We evaluate our method using the INFINITY-CHAT dataset on state-of-the-art open weight models under $\sim$20B parameters. Our results demonstrate a significant reduction in semantic convergence, with average pairwise cosine similarity dropping from ($\approx 0.85$) to ($\approx 0.65$). Our scheme achieves a majority of questions below the 0.7 threshold, effectively reducing the gap between artificial mode collapse and human-level typological diversity. We provide our implementation as an open-source framework to enable more diverse and creative AI deployments.

</details>


### [2] [GeoID-PINN: Identifiability-Aware Regional Epidemic Inference with Geographic Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02633)
*Weixiong Hua,Fan Bu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出GeoID-PINN，一种用于SIRD动力学的物理信息神经网络，通过行随机源组成矩阵建模区域间依赖，并引入空间先验正则化；模拟和真实数据实验表明其能提升区域依赖结构恢复精度和预测准确性，但轨迹拟合良好不代表依赖结构可唯一识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域监测数据混合了本地传播、报告偏差、输入病例和外部感染压力，难以区分；现有模型难以准确恢复区域间依赖结构，且轨迹拟合优良可能掩盖结构识别问题。

Method: GeoID-PINN结合SIRD微分方程与神经网络，使用行随机源组成矩阵表示区域间感染来源权重，并通过距离、邻接、通勤或先行-滞后信息构造空间先验进行正则化；评估中使用模拟数据和路易斯安那州64县COVID-19数据进行回顾性验证，并对比自回归负二项基线。

Result: 四区域模拟中，兼容距离先验的源组成误差为0.099，无正则化升至0.159，强错误先验达0.577，但轨迹拟合和传播尺度估计相似；COVID-19数据中，预测训练的Geo-PINN将MSE从32957降至11468，MAE从70.60降至57.73，但基线NLL更低（5.158 vs 5.346）；15县对照中，邻接先验使MSE降低6.85%，MAE降低3.1%。

Conclusion: 结构化正则化能改善依赖结构恢复和点预测精度，但不同合理先验下性能相近，表明无法唯一确定区域间边；结果需先进行先验敏感性和观测模型检验后再作解释。

Abstract: Regional surveillance data reflect local transmission, reporting, seeding, and external infection pressure, which are difficult to identify separately. We introduce GeoID-PINN, a physics-informed neural network (PINN) for susceptible-infectious-recovered-deceased (SIRD) dynamics. The model represents spatial dependence with a row-stochastic source-composition matrix whose rows assign nonnegative source weights that sum to one. We regularize this matrix toward a spatial prior constructed from distance, adjacency, commuting, or lead-lag information. In a four-region simulation with known truth, a compatible distance prior gives source-composition error 0.099. The error rises to 0.159 without regularization and 0.577 under a strongly misspecified prior, while trajectory fit and transmission-scale estimates remain similar. Accurate trajectories therefore do not guarantee recovery of the regional dependence structure. We also evaluate GeoID-PINN retrospectively using COVID-19 data from 64 Louisiana counties. Relative to an autoregressive negative-binomial baseline, Forecast-Trained Geo-PINN reduces mean squared error (MSE) from 32,957 to 11,468 and mean absolute error (MAE) from 70.60 to 57.73. The baseline has lower negative log likelihood (NLL), 5.158 versus 5.346, indicating better distributional fit but worse point accuracy. In a controlled 15-county comparison, county adjacency reduces MSE by 6.85 percent and MAE by 3.1 percent. Similar performance across plausible priors supports structured regularization but not unique edge recovery. These results require prior-sensitivity and observation-model checks before interpretation.

</details>


### [3] [HyperAgent: Planning and Acting over Tool-Schema Hypergraphs for Tool-Use LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02650)
*Zian Zhai,Xingyu Tan,Gaowang Zou,Xiaoyang Wang,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出基于工具-模式超图的HyperAgent框架，通过动态规划与执行提升LLM智能体的工具使用可靠性，减少冗余调用和资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体依赖外部工具完成复杂任务，但现有方法基于文本描述隐式推理工具组合，导致效率低且不可靠；工具间关系及执行环境动态性未被显式建模。

Method: 构建工具-模式超图（工具为超边，连接输入/输出模式节点），任务时提取相关子图生成模式感知的任务DAG，执行中通过缺陷导向扩展动态构建状态条件工具支持图，识别未满足需求并检索生产者工具。

Result: 在AppWorld上，HyperAgent提升任务完成性能，同时减少API调用、LLM交互次数和令牌消耗，优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 显式建模工具关系并动态规划执行可显著提升工具使用智能体的效率与可靠性，验证超图引导框架的有效性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on external tools to complete complex real-world tasks. However, reliable tool-use planning remains challenging due to the limitations of implicit reasoning and the evolving nature of real-world execution environments. Existing tool-use agents typically rely on LLMs to infer tool compositions from textual descriptions, which can lead to inefficient exploration and unreliable execution in complex tasks. To address these challenges, we model tool relations at the schema level and construct a directed Tool--Schema Hypergraph, in which tools are represented as hyperedges from their required input-schema nodes to their output-schema nodes. Furthermore, we propose HyperAgent, a Tool--Schema Hypergraph-guided framework for dynamic planning and execution. Given a task, HyperAgent first extracts a task-relevant tool context graph and uses it to guide the construction of a schema-aware Task DAG. During execution, HyperAgent dynamically realizes each subtask by constructing a state-conditioned tool support graph through deficit-oriented expansion, which identifies unresolved requirements and retrieves supporting producer tools according to the current agent state. Experiments on AppWorld demonstrate that HyperAgent improves task completion performance while reducing redundant API calls, LLM interactions, and token consumption compared with existing agent baselines.

</details>


### [4] [Sphere Retraction Normalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02668)
*Jie Zhang,Cheng-Fang Su,Yi-Jui Huang,Min-Te Sun*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出p-SpheretNorm，一种将残差连接统一在球面流形上的单参数角回缩框架，其中GeoNorm和Euclidean残差仅为极端情况，有限p值在nanoGPT上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残差连接是训练深层网络的标准机制，GeoNorm通过黎曼指数映射将残差流限制在超球面上，但指数映射只是回缩映射族的一员，并非最优选择，需要探索其他回缩方式以提升性能。

Method: 将残差连接和GeoNorm统一为球面上的标量角度转换框架，提出度量投影回缩（Proj-SpheretNorm）和Cayley回缩（Cay-SpheretNorm），两者均为p=1和p=2的特例，构成单参数族p-SpheretNorm，其旋转角饱和而非无限增长。

Result: 在nanoGPT上，Proj、Cay和p-SpheretNorm均优于现有轻量连接方案，最佳验证损失出现在有限p值（而非指数映射对应的极限），表明指数映射不是优选。

Conclusion: 指数映射仅是球面残差流回缩谱系的端点，有限角度饱和的回缩更有效，p-SpheretNorm提供更优且计算简单的替代方案。

Abstract: Residual connections are the de facto mechanism for training deep neural networks stably. Geodesic Normalization (GeoNorm) recasts them on a Riemannian manifold, orthogonalizing each layer output against the current hidden state and applying the resulting update through the Riemannian exponential map. Every hidden state thus keeps a constant $\ell_{2}$-norm, confining the residual stream to a hypersphere. The exponential map, however, is only one member of a broad family of retraction maps. We show that on the hypersphere this entire family collapses to a single scalar design choice. What distinguishes one retraction from another is only how the magnitude of an update is converted into a rotation angle within the plane spanned by the hidden state and the update. This view places Euclidean residual connections and GeoNorm in one framework. Instantiating it with the metric projection retraction and the Cayley retraction yields Proj-SpheretNorm and Cay-SpheretNorm, which are exactly norm-preserving yet require only algebraic operations. Both prove to be members of a one-parameter family of angular retractions, $p$-SpheretNorm, whose rotation angle saturates rather than growing without bound. The two methods above are recovered exactly at $p = 1$ and $p = 2$, while the identity map and GeoNorm arise only as limits at either end. On nanoGPT, all three methods outperform existing lightweight deep connection schemes, and the best validation loss is attained at finite $p$, indicating that the exponential map is not the preferred retraction for spherical residual streams but merely one end of a spectrum.

</details>


### [5] [Predictive Set Theory: A Generative Framework for Cognitive Architecture with Operationalized Core Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02704)
*Yiyang Yu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出预测集合论（PST），从第一性原理出发，以极简操作集（传感器、状态刷新、三种指称链）形式化重构认知架构，推导出状态序列、需求、比较、效率及有限视界概率规划等核心功能，并宣称能解决罗素悖论、哥德尔不完备性认知地位、负反馈奠基及电影剪辑理解等经典问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测加工理论缺乏对“预测”结构、预测误差标准响应及更新一致性的操作性定义；贝叶斯认知科学预设封闭假设空间，且无法生成概率对象的离散指称。两者均缺乏生成性基础。

Method: PST以集合论为基底，定义传感器为恒等函数，引入集合论状态刷新，并构建参考、反参考、半参考三种指称链，从这些最小操作中严格推导认知功能，作为任何在不完全信息与不可逆风险下保持内部一致的系统的设计规范。

Result: 框架推导出状态序列、需求、比较、效率及有限视界概率规划；对罗素悖论、哥德尔不完全性、负反馈接地及电影剪辑理解提供新解释；论文主要目的为通过公开学术记录确立PST的原创性与完备性。

Conclusion: PST旨在作为认知架构的生成性设计规范而非神经机制模型，其价值在于提供一套从基础操作到高级功能的逻辑一致框架，并公开声明其理论贡献的原创性与完整性。

Abstract: Predictive processing theories portray the brain as a hierarchical prediction engine that minimizes prediction error, yet they lack operational definitions for the structure of a "prediction," the standardized response to a prediction error, and the mechanism that maintains consistency across successive updates. Bayesian cognitive science attempts to subsume all uncertainty under probabilistic belief updating, but it presupposes a closed hypothesis space and provides no generative account of how the objects over which probabilities are distributed become discrete, identifiable referents in the first place. This paper introduces Predictive Set Theory (PST), a formal generative framework that reconstructs cognitive architecture from first principles. PST anchors cognition in a minimal set of operations---a sensor formalized as an identity function, set-theoretic state refresh, and three fundamental forms of reference chains (reference, counter-reference, and semi-reference)---and rigorously derives core cognitive functions including state sequences, demand, comparison, efficiency, and finite-horizon probabilistic planning. Rather than modeling neural mechanisms, PST constitutes a design specification for any system that must maintain internal consistency while acting under incomplete information and irreversible risk. The framework offers novel resolutions to classical problems such as Russell's paradox, the cognitive status of Gödelian incompleteness, the grounding of negative feedback, and the comprehension of film editing. The primary purpose of this paper is to establish, through the public academic record, the originality and completeness of the Predictive Set Theory framework.

</details>


### [6] [Learning Molecular Representations from Cellular Phenotypes with Structure Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02688)
*Xuan Lin,Jingyu Sheng,Tengfei Ma,Li Sun,Dapeng Xiong*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出 PhenMol 框架，通过解耦分子与细胞表征的共享/私有成分，在保持化学结构邻域组织的同时实现表型引导对齐，在多个任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态学习方法仅优化对齐而忽视化学空间内在结构，导致分子表征扭曲和结构信息丢失。

Method: PhenMol 将分子和细胞表征分解为共享与私有组件，通过专用分子分支保留化学结构，实现表型引导对齐且不破坏邻域关系。

Result: 在约3.04×10^4个分子-细胞形态对上，改进270个生物活性任务的属性预测、分子-表型检索及临床试验结果预测；ECFP4分析显示其更好保留分子邻域并降低嵌入畸变。

Conclusion: 结构感知约束对多模态分子表示学习至关重要，该框架为将细胞表型与化学知识整合用于药物发现提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Phenotypic drug discovery enables the discovery of functional relationships between molecular structures and cellular responses. However, existing multimodal representation learning methods often optimize cross-modal alignment without considering the intrinsic organization of chemical space, resulting in distorted molecular representations and loss of structural information. We propose \textbf{PhenMol}, a structure-preserving framework for phenotype-aware molecular representation learning. PhenMol disentangles molecular and cellular representations into shared and private components, enabling phenotype-guided alignment while preserving chemical structures through a dedicated molecular branch. This design integrates cellular phenotype information without disrupting molecular neighborhood organization. Experiments on approximately $3.04 \times 10^{4}$ molecule--cell morphology pairs demonstrate that PhenMol improves molecular property prediction across 270 bioactivity tasks, molecule--phenotype retrieval, and clinical trial outcome prediction. Moreover, ECFP4-based structural analysis shows that PhenMol better preserves molecular neighborhoods and reduces embedding distortion compared with existing multimodal alignment methods. These results highlight the importance of structure-aware constraints in multimodal molecular representation learning and provide an effective approach for integrating cellular phenotypes with chemical knowledge for drug discovery.

</details>


### [7] [Towards a new paradigm of scientific discovery with socialized artificial intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02775)
*Xinjie Yao,Xingxin Xu,Xiyuan Gao,Zhoupeng Guo,Kunlong Yang,Dengyu Zhao,Siqi Zhao,Zhihe Fan,Yichen Dong,Xin Li,Jiekang Feng,Jiahe Wu,Sen Wang,Beiming Yu,Kejia Zhao,Ruipu Zhao,Jiaqi Zhou,Heyang Li,Jianjun Chen,Anbo Dai,Xin Liu,Zhengtao Yu,Qinghua Hu,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出BLAZE（连接文献、智能体与零间隙实验）范式，将AI从孤立任务助手转变为科学发现的组织基础设施，通过持续整合知识、推理、实验与人类判断，构建社会化科学智能，使发现过程更具可追溯性、可复现性和累积性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现已历经经验、理论、计算和数据驱动等阶段，当前核心挑战不再是信息生产，而是如何将不断膨胀的知识、推理与证据组织为连贯的发现过程；现有AI辅助模式局限于碎片化任务，缺乏系统性整合。

Method: BLAZE将AI视为科学发现的组织化基础设施，连接持久知识库、集体推理、经验验证与人类判断，形成持续的研究生命周期；强调科学智能源于知识、假设、实验与集体验证间的持续交互，而非单纯计算。

Result: 通过人与机器在共享科学过程中的组织协同，BLAZE使发现更可追溯、可复现、可累积，同时保留人类创造力、判断力与责任，扩展集体科学探究的规模、深度与连续性。

Conclusion: 社会化科学智能可能成为下一代科学的基础，其目的不是取代人类发现，而是延伸集体科学探究的能力边界。

Abstract: Scientific discovery has advanced through successive transformations in the organization of knowledge. Observation and experimentation established the empirical foundations of science. Theory made it possible to derive general principles from particular phenomena. Computation extended inquiry into systems beyond direct observation, while data-intensive methods opened new spaces of pattern and prediction. Science now confronts a different frontier. The central challenge is no longer simply to produce more information, but to organize expanding knowledge, reasoning, and evidence into a coherent process of discovery. Here, we introduce Bridging Literature, Agents, and Zero-gap Experimentation (BLAZE), a paradigm of socialized scientific intelligence. BLAZE conceives AI not as an assistant for isolated research tasks, but as an organizational infrastructure for scientific discovery. It connects persistent knowledge, collective reasoning, empirical validation, and human judgment within a continuous research lifecycle, transforming fragmented activities into a cumulative process of inquiry, criticism, and revision. The central premise of BLAZE is that scientific intelligence does not arise from computation alone. It emerges from the sustained interaction among knowledge, hypotheses, experiments, and collective verification. By organizing humans and machines within a shared scientific process, BLAZE makes discovery more traceable, reproducible, and cumulative while preserving human creativity, judgment, and responsibility. Socialized scientific intelligence may provide a foundation for the next era of science. Its purpose is not to replace human discovery, but to extend the scale, depth, and continuity of collective scientific inquiry.

</details>


### [8] [GLOBE: Trajectory-Aligned Gradient Matching with Structured SparseOptimization for Coreset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02690)
*Hetian Liu,Jin Cui,Mengcheng Shi,Yanbin Hu,Xinyue Long,Boran Zhao,Pengju Pen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出GLOBE框架，通过梯度轨迹与多阶匹配实现全局优化的核心集选择，在低保留率下显著提升下游测试精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于梯度的核心集方法依赖单快照梯度且采用贪心选择，难以捕捉优化动态并处理强相关样本。

Method: 利用多检查点梯度轨迹表征样本，联合对齐一阶均值与投影非中心二阶矩，结合Group LASSO、弹性网和非负预算约束实现结构稀疏，最后进行类平衡Top-K选择。

Result: 在六个基准和五种架构上，GLOBE一致优于现有方法，尤其在低保留率下优势明显。

Conclusion: 动态梯度信息、多阶分布匹配与结构稀疏性相结合能有效提升数据高效学习性能。

Abstract: On-device training of deep neural networks is fundamentally constrained by the computational and memory costs of large-scale datasets. Coreset selection offers a practical solution by retaining only a compact subset of real training samples. However, existing gradient-based methods commonly rely on gradients computed at a single model snapshot and employ greedy or pursuit-based selection procedures, limiting their ability to capture evolving optimization dynamics and handle strongly correlated samples. We propose GLOBE (Gradient Local-Balanced Extraction), a trajectory-aligned coreset selection framework that formulates sample selection as a globally optimized sparse weighting problem. GLOBE represents each sample by a gradient trajectory constructed across multiple training checkpoints, thereby capturing its influence throughout different stages of optimization. To preserve the training behavior of the full dataset, we introduce a multi-order matching objective that jointly aligns the first-order mean and projected uncentered second-order moments of gradient trajectories. GLOBE further combines Group LASSO, Elastic Net regularization, and nonnegative budget constraints to induce group- and sample-level sparsity while stabilizing the weights of correlated trajectories. Finally, class-balanced Top-K selection maintains adequate category coverage under limited sampling budgets. Experiments across six benchmarks and five evaluation architectures demonstrate that GLOBE consistently outperforms existing coreset selection methods in downstream test accuracy, particularly at low retention ratios. These results highlight the effectiveness of combining dynamic gradient information, multi-order distribution matching, and structured sparsity for data-efficient learning.

</details>


### [9] [BAP-SQL: Budget-Aware Observation Planning for Agentic Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02876)
*Chong Peng,Pin Qian,Su Wang,Yihang Chen,Varun Sah*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出BAP-SQL，通过在观察形成阶段进行预算控制，显著提升了智能体文本到SQL任务中严格预算下的成功率，同时减少了token使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体在文本到SQL任务中，其动作决定后续观察，但广泛查询会浪费上下文和数据库资源，事后压缩无法弥补。现有方法缺乏对观察形成的预算控制，导致在严格预算下性能下降。

Method: BAP-SQL将观察形成视为预算控制阶段，包含三个部分：估计查询风险、在有用时重写SQL、以及通过独立运行时防护罩强制执行硬限制。

Result: 在BIRD衍生基准上，BAP-SQL相对于匹配的SFT提升了3.4/3.6个百分点，同时减少了4.5/5.0%的token使用；收益在较小模型和严格预算下更明显，但在最宽松设置下出现反转，且未减少数据库工作。

Conclusion: BAP-SQL通过预算敏感的规划和救援机制有效提升了严格预算下的性能，但收益随模型能力和预算增加而减弱，甚至在最宽松情况下逆转，表明预算控制策略需要根据具体场景调整。

Abstract: Tool-using agents do not merely consume observations: their actions determine what arrives next. In agentic text-to-SQL, a broad query can spend context and database work before useful evidence appears, while post-hoc compression cannot recover omitted rows or expended work. We present BAP-SQL, which treats observation formation as a budget-control stage: it estimates query risk, rewrites SQL when useful, and delegates hard limits to an independent runtime shield. Across general 4B, specialized FINER-SQL 4B, and 7B backbones, BAP-SQL improves tight-budget success. On the primary BIRD-derived setting, it gains 3.4/3.6 percentage points over matched SFT while using 4.5/5.0% fewer tokens. Matched retraining and task-level transfer associate the gain with policy-visible planning and budget-sensitive rescue. The benefit attenuates as model capability and budget increase, reverses at the loosest setting, and does not reduce database work.

</details>


### [10] [Output-Aware Rotation for INT2 KV-Cache Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02691)
*Vincent-Daniel Yun,Woosang Lim,Minsoo Cheong,Sunwoo Lee,Murali Annavaram,Sai Praneeth Karimireddy,Sungjoo Yoo*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出输出感知旋转方法OptR，通过最小化经过W_O投影后的注意力输出误差来改进INT2量化KV缓存，在保持分页KV缓存格式和极低推理开销的同时，显著提升长上下文推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有旋转式INT2量化方法仅优化缓存统计量或代理误差，未考虑误差经过注意力机制和输出投影W_O后的真实传播影响，导致量化优化目标与最终模型性能失配。

Method: 将W_O后的注意力输出误差分解为键相关和值相关两项，在每个注意力头上学习正交旋转校正；进一步通过等价于注意力的键重参数化降低大通道偏移而不改变softmax分布，全程经过完整INT2量化和注意力路径优化。

Result: 在三个模型和五个推理/编码基准上，OptR一致优于QuaRot和OSCAR基线，并增强了长上下文检索性能，同时保持分页KV缓存格式且推理开销可忽略。

Conclusion: 输出感知的量化优化比单纯优化缓存统计量更有效，OptR为超低位宽KV缓存量化提供了新的范式，兼顾精度、通用性和部署友好性。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache has become a major memory and bandwidth bottleneck in long-context large language model inference, making ultra-low-bit quantization increasingly important. However, existing rotation-based INT2 methods optimize cache statistics or proxy errors before the complete attention readout, even though the model is ultimately affected by the error propagated through attention and the output projection $W_O$. To address this mismatch, we propose \textit{OptR}, an output-aware rotation method that minimizes post-$W_O$ attention-output error. OptR decomposes the post-$W_O$ attention-output error into key- and value-induced terms and learns per-head orthogonal corrections through the full INT2 quantization and attention path. OptR further applies an attention-equivalent key reparameterization to reduce large channel-wise offsets without changing the softmax distribution. Across three models and five reasoning and coding benchmarks, OptR consistently improves both QuaRot and OSCAR and strengthens long-context retrieval, while preserving the paged KV-cache format with negligible inference overhead.

</details>


### [11] [Interpreting Black-Box Large Language Models with Sentence-Level Energy Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02879)
*Maryam Rezaee,Pooriya Safaei,Maryam Asgarinezhad,Fatemeh Seyyedsalehi*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种模型无关、事后、句子级别的归因解释器，通过训练能量模型作为代理，在无需额外API查询的情况下量化提示句子对目标输出的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭源大型语言模型通过API提供，缺乏内部可解释性，导致负责任部署面临关键挑战。

Method: 训练能量模型作为替代模型，捕捉提示与响应间的概念一致性；利用该能量景观训练轻量级解释器网络，该解释器为独立工具，训练后无需再调用LLM即可评估提示句子的影响，并在全局多样输入上训练以缓解实例偏差。

Result: 实验表明能量模型能准确模拟目标LLM，解释器能有效识别对生成特定目标输出最具影响的提示句子。

Conclusion: 该框架通过全局训练局部解释器，捕获更广泛的生成模式，为闭源LLM提供可行的解释途径。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of proprietary Large Language Models (LLMs) accessed strictly through closed APIs has created a critical challenge for responsible deployment: a fundamental lack of interpretability. To address this, we propose a model-agnostic, post-hoc attribution interpreter operating at the sentence level. Our approach trains an Energy-Based Model (EBM) as a surrogate to capture the LLM's internal conceptual consistency between prompts and responses. This energy landscape guides the training of a lightweight interpreter network. Uniquely, our interpreter operates as a standalone tool; once trained, it quantifies the influence of prompt sentences on a user-specified target output without requiring further API queries to the LLM. By globally training a local interpreter across diverse inputs, our framework captures broader generation patterns and mitigates instance-specific biases. Experiments demonstrate that our EBM accurately simulates the target LLM, allowing the interpreter to effectively identify the prompt sentences most influential in generating specific target outputs.

</details>


### [12] [Steganalysis of Adaptive Covert Collusion in Tool-Using Agent Populations: A Black-Box, Cross-Principal Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02698)
*Mohamed Chahine Ghanem*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 针对LLM多智能体共享基础设施中的隐蔽协作风险，提出一种仅基于行为观测的黑盒隐写检测器，并通过红蓝对抗评估其能力边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单智能体安全措施无法防范多个智能体在共享环境中通过隐蔽通信协同操纵市场、刷分或联合窃取数据，且组织间无法查看彼此模型，检测只能依赖黑盒行为痕迹。

Method: 将隐蔽协作建模为信息隐藏问题，构建结合互信息估计、置换检验、分布偏移统计及时序/工具调用侧信道的黑盒隐写检测器，并通过持续重写编码的对抗者进行红蓝对抗测试。

Result: 利用容量理论预测检测-容量前沿（低于某隐蔽比特率则检测无法超越随机猜测），并报告了占位性实验结果，同时指出跨会话载荷分散这一现有方法难以应对的规避手段。

Conclusion: 黑盒检测存在根本性能力下限，但通过对抗训练可逼近该边界；需警惕跨会话分散载荷等高级规避策略，未来工作应聚焦于该前沿的实证映射与检测鲁棒性提升。

Abstract: Tool-using agents built on large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed not by a single operator but by many, side by side on shared infrastructure. This creates a population-level risk that single-agent safeguards miss: a handful of agents can quietly coordinate, rigging a market, boosting one another in a review process, or timing a joint data grab, while each one looks perfectly well-behaved. The difficulty is that the organisations running these agents cannot see inside one another's models, so any realistic detector must work from behaviour alone: black-box, trace-only, and often with only partial visibility. We treat covert coordination as an information-hiding problem and build a black-box steganalysis detector that combines cross-run mutual-information estimation, permutation tests, distributional-shift statistics, and timing and tool-call side channels, all calibrated to a fixed false-positive budget. Our central move is to stop testing against a single fixed code: we pit the detector against an adversary that continually rewrites its encoding to slip past whatever the detector has learned, and we run this red-versus-blue contest in tool-using, memory-carrying environments rather than toy games. Capacity theory then tells us what to expect, a detection-capacity frontier, a covert bit-rate below which black-box detection is provably no better than chance. We set out an experiment to map this frontier, report clearly labelled placeholder results pending measurement, and flag a practical evasion, spreading a payload across sessions, that current methods largely miss.

</details>


### [13] [NANQ: Noise-Floor-Aware Mixed-Precision Non-Uniform Quantization for Analog Compute-in-Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02700)
*Yizhe Chen,Wenshuai Yao,Saiya Wang,Yuannuo Feng,Wenbo Qi,Kechao Tang,Ngai Wong,Wenyong Zhou,Wang Kang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出NANQ，一种面向模拟CIM的噪声感知混合精度非均匀量化框架，通过建模硬件噪声并自适应分配量化精度，在eFlash CIM SoC上显著提升低比特量化模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CIM量化方法仅最小化理想量化误差，忽略硬件噪声底限，导致精度分配低效，无法应对器件变化和读噪声对低比特模型的严重退化。

Method: NANQ基于eFlash CIM阵列实测响应建模幅值相关权重噪声，将噪声分布转换为自适应量化密度（低噪声区域细量化，噪声主导区域避免无效精度）；并通过统一阈值识别各层在硬件噪声下的精度饱和点，分配层间比特宽度。

Result: 在2比特权重幅值量化下，NANQ在视觉模型上平均提升8.05个百分点准确率，语言模型PPL平均降低54.7%（对比PowerQuant）；混合精度仅需3.2-3.8等效比特即可捕获大部分增益。

Conclusion: NANQ有效利用硬件噪声特征指导量化精度分配，在模拟CIM上实现更优的精度-资源权衡，验证了噪声感知量化的实用性和高效性。

Abstract: Analog compute-in-memory (CIM) enables energy-efficient neural network inference, but device variation and read noise can severely degrade low-bit quantized models. Existing CIM-oriented quantization methods mainly minimize ideal quantization error, ignoring the hardware noise floor and thus causing inefficient precision allocation. We propose NANQ, a noise-aware mixed-precision non-uniform quantization framework for analog CIM. NANQ models magnitude-dependent weight noise from measured responses of an eFlash CIM array and converts the noise profile into an adaptive quantization density, assigning finer resolution to low-noise regions while avoiding ineffective precision in noise-dominated regions. It further assigns layer-wise bit-widths by identifying each layer's precision saturation point under hardware noise using a unified threshold. On-chip experiments on an eFlash CIM SoC show that, under 2-bit weight-magnitude quantization, NANQ improves vision-model accuracy by 8.05 percentage points and reduces language-model PPL by 54.7% on average over PowerQuant. Mixed-precision NANQ captures most of the gains obtainable from additional quantization resources with only 3.2-3.8 equivalent bits.

</details>


### [14] [Privacy-Preserving AI Verification via Minimal Information Disclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02774)
*Sleem Abdelghafar,Gabriel Kulp*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出最小信息披露（MID）框架，量化验证过程中证据的附带泄露，在保持验证准确性的同时最小化隐私损失，并通过多种物理测量与任务验证其有效性及ZKP兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI验证涉及信任边界问题：验证者需获取足够信息以验证声明，但同一证据可能泄露模型、工作负载或硬件的敏感细节。现有方法缺乏对证据本身信息内容的系统量化与设计，因此需要一种通用框架来度量并最小化验证过程中的附带信息泄露。

Method: MID框架利用条件互信息量化附带泄露，即已知授权结果后释放信息仍泄露的受保护属性信息。其设计具有通用性，可适配不同验证目标、保护属性、证据来源及部署约束；通过三个机制设计变量（证据通道、采集策略、释放变换）实现机制实例化，并支持ZKP认证的释放（如Groth16 zk-SNARK证明线性投影机制）。

Result: 在四项物理测量和六项验证任务（执行类型、硬件身份、计算规模、模型身份）中，MID实现三种完美留出验证且零附带泄露的释放；其余任务产生明确的隐私-效用前沿。同时，所提线性投影机制可被Groth16 zk-SNARK高效认证。

Conclusion: MID提供了一种通用且实用的验证隐私量化与设计方法，能同时实现高验证准确性与低信息泄露，并可扩展至ZKP等可部署机制，为AI验证中的隐私保护提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: AI verification crosses a trust boundary: a verifier must learn enough to establish an authorized claim, yet the same evidence can reveal sensitive details about the model, workload, or hardware. We introduce minimal information disclosure (MID), which designs and quantifies the information content of verifier-facing evidence itself. MID measures collateral leakage with conditional mutual information: what the release reveals about the protected property after the authorized result is known. MID is general by design: it can accommodate different verification goals, protected properties, evidence sources, and deployment constraints. To demonstrate MID's practicality, we evaluate it on four physical measurements and six verification tasks spanning execution type, hardware identity, compute scale, and model identity. These experiments use three mechanism-design variables--the evidence channel, collection policy, and release transformation--but MID is not limited to these choices and can accommodate other deployable mechanisms. Across these tasks, MID produces three releases with perfect held-out verification and zero measured collateral leakage, while the remaining tasks yield explicit privacy--utility frontiers. MID also supports ZKP-certified releases: we demonstrate our proposed linear-projection mechanism using a Groth16 zk-SNARK.

</details>


### [15] [Designing a Good Virtual Node: Addressable and Cardinality-Preserving Global Memory for Message Passing Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02709)
*Félix Marcoccia*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 通过可寻址的交叉注意力槽位和私有键值锚点, 提出了保持基数信息的虚拟内存模块, 以缓解消息传递神经网络中标准虚拟节点管道的容量瓶颈, 计算复杂度为O(nMd).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准虚拟节点将图压缩为单一同质状态并广播给所有节点, 导致有限容量瓶颈; 现有方法依赖自注意力, 但希望探索无需自注意力的辅助虚拟内存方案.

Method: 首先将全局内存分解为独立可读写状态的地址化交叉注意力槽位; 其次, 针对softmax注意力对均匀复制不变的问题, 为每个槽位查询插入私有键值锚点, 恢复被丢弃的归一化质量, 从而在有限颜色域上实现内射多集表示并可执行1-WL细化.

Result: 在考虑多重性的Two-Radius任务、子图计数任务和受限链接集预测任务上验证了方法的有效性, 并达到O(nMd)的算术复杂度.

Conclusion: 可寻址且保留基数的虚拟内存可以以线性成本缓解标准虚拟节点的信息瓶颈, 是自注意力之外的一种有效且高效的全局通信替代方案.

Abstract: Virtual nodes give message-passing neural networks a simple global communication route, but the standard node--VN--node pipeline compresses the graph into one homogeneous state and broadcasts it identically to every node. Building on the Two-Radius analysis of Mishayev et al., we ask how auxiliary virtual memory can relieve this finite-capacity bottleneck without self-attention. We identify two requirements. First, the global memory should be factorized into independently writable and readable states: this can be achieved using addressable cross-attention slots. Second, addressability alone does not preserve multiplicity, because softmax attention is invariant to uniform replication. Inserting each slot query as a private key/value anchor recovers the discarded normalization mass and yields, on bounded color domains, an injective multiset representation able to implement a 1-WL refinement. Experiments on multiplicity-aware Two-Radius, motif counting, and constrained link-set prediction support this addressable and cardinality-preserving virtual memory at (O(nMd)) arithmetic cost.

</details>


### [16] [Neurosymbolic Reasoning with Incremental Knowledge for Sample Efficient Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02993)
*Subrat Prasad Panda,Blaise Genest,Arvind Easwaran*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出神经符号分层强化学习框架InK，通过可更新的符号知识与低层神经网络结合，显著提升稀疏奖励导航任务的样本效率，并开发了信念世界树搜索算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分层强化学习中的知识是固定且不可更新的，无法利用探索中获得的增量知识，导致在稀疏奖励、长程推理环境中样本效率低下。

Method: 高层符号组件在可更新的知识表示上进行符号规划（如D*），低层目标条件神经网络通过奖励塑形学习运动原语，同时提出信念世界树搜索以利用先验知识进行最优规划。

Result: 在导航任务上的实验表明，融入增量知识显著提升了样本效率。

Conclusion: 神经符号HRL结合增量知识是提升复杂稀疏奖励环境下RL样本效率的有效途径，开源代码便于复现和扩展。

Abstract: (Flat) Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents face significant challenges in environments with sparse rewards that require long-horizon reasoning. A compelling approach to improve sample efficiency is to incorporate knowledge into learning and decision-making. In standard Hierarchical RL (HRL), knowledge is encoded in a fixed, non-updatable form, such as architectural choices, and remains unchanged throughout learning. With fixed HRL, reasoning with incremental knowledge learned during exploration is impractical before sufficient environmental knowledge is acquired, leading to poor sample efficiency. In this work, we propose neurosymbolic HRL with {\em Incremental Knowledge (InK)}: symbolic high-level components perform {\em symbolic planning} (e.g. using $D^*$) on an updatable representation of current InK, while low-level goal-conditioned neural modules learn motion primitives through experience using reward shaping. Experiments on navigation tasks demonstrate that incorporating InK substantially improves sample efficiency. Additionally, to perform {\em optimal} symbolic planning given {\em prior} knowledge about the world, we develop Belief World Tree Search. The code is available at https://github.com/CPS-research-group/ink_bwts.

</details>


### [17] [Evaluation Blindness: How Silent Measurement Failures Corrupt AI Systems from Training to Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02786)
*Priyanka Bajaj*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出"评估盲区"（evaluation blindness）概念，指测量函数在系统实际故障时仍产生与健康状态无差异的读数，且无辅助信号提示差距。该问题在训练与部署两阶段均存在，并通过形式化可检测性谓词、四例训练案例、六类故障分类法及对50起真实事件的验证，表明53%的可验证公共故障是静默的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统可能静默失效，且问题贯穿训练循环、评估管道与生产监控，直到下游危害显现才被察觉。现有文献将训练与部署阶段分开处理，缺乏统一框架，导致此类静默故障未被系统性认识与应对。

Method: 提出形式化可检测性谓词统一两阶段；通过四例训练时案例（包括TRL PR #6594真实梯度损坏bug）揭示具体失效机制；构建六类生产故障分类法，并基于法院文件与监管 filings 中的50起真实事件验证；引入失败预算框架，将可接受失败率与用例风险等级关联。

Result: 六类分类法验证显示53%的可验证公共故障是静默的，其中 Operational 类在结构定义上100%静默。训练案例证明即便损失正常下降，梯度仍可能被悄然破坏。

Conclusion: 测量基础设施应被视为贯穿AI全生命周期的正确性关切，而非仅评估阶段的问题。数据、代码与分类法已开源。

Abstract: AI systems can fail silently. The failure propagates through training loops, evaluation pipelines, and production monitoring stacks until downstream harm makes it visible. This paper introduces evaluation blindness: a measurement function M exhibits evaluation blindness with respect to failure class F when it produces readings indistinguishable from a healthy state while the system is actually failing, with no auxiliary signal flagging the gap.
  The problem surfaces at two lifecycle stages the literature has treated separately. At training time, reward models are gamed, importance-sampling corrections are silently miscalculated, and benchmark contamination inflates fine-tuning evaluations, all while loss curves look healthy and gradient updates proceed normally. At deployment time, monitoring fails to catch six classes of production failure, including an Operational category that is 100% silent by structural definition.
  We provide a formal detectability predicate unifying both stages. Four training-time case studies trace concrete breakdowns, including a real implementation bug in TRL PR #6594 where gradients are corrupted as loss decreases normally. A six-class taxonomy validated against 50 real-world incidents from court documents and regulatory filings finds that 53% of verifiable public failures were silent. A failure budget framework ties acceptable failure rates to use-case risk class.
  The implication is direct: measurement infrastructure is a correctness concern across the full AI lifecycle, not just at evaluation time. Data, code, and taxonomy schema are at https://github.com/priyanka25aug/llm-failure-taxonomy.

</details>


### [18] [ProPRL: Property-Aware Prerequisite Relation Learning in Educational Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03006)
*Xinghe Cheng,Jiapu Wang,Chaobo He,Ruihai Dong,Quanlong Guan*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出ProPRL框架，通过属性感知表示、自适应门控融合和不可逆约束，提升先修关系学习的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将先修关系学习视为常规链接预测，无法自适应整合互补教育证据，且难以抑制矛盾的反向预测。

Method: 利用概念-资源超图和有向学习行为图学习互补表示，采用方向保持的个性化传播聚合多跳行为证据，通过Pair-conditioned Gate自适应加权融合两种视图，并引入Irreversibility Constraint正则化惩罚双向高置信度。

Result: 在多个真实教育数据集上达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: ProPRL有效改进了先修关系学习，验证了属性感知、自适应融合与不可逆约束的联合作用。

Abstract: Prerequisite relation learning is central to adaptive instruction, yet existing methods often formulate it as conventional link prediction, limiting their ability to adaptively integrate complementary educational evidence for individual candidate pairs and to discourage contradictory reverse predictions. We propose ProPRL, a Property-aware Prerequisite Relation Learning framework. ProPRL first learns complementary concept representations from a concept-resource hypergraph and a directed learning-behavior graph, where direction-preserving personalized propagation aggregates multi-hop behavioral evidence. It then employs a Pair-conditioned Gate to adaptively weight and fuse the two views for each candidate ordered concept pair. Finally, an \textit{Irreversibility Constraint} introduces an anti-symmetry regularizer that penalizes simultaneously high confidence in both directions of the same concept pair. Experiments on multiple real-world educational datasets show that ProPRL achieves state-of-the-art performance on prerequisite relation learning.

</details>


### [19] [Topological Simplification in Predictive Coding Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02816)
*Adam Shaw,Jiayu Li,Michael Sperling,Michael Kim,Alvin Jin*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文利用持续同调分析预测编码网络（PCN）的层间拓扑变化，发现小模型更早压缩连通分量，压缩时机与重构误差负相关，且PCN比同规模MLP更晚压缩，表明模型容量和双向动力学共同影响压缩-重构权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经启发式双向架构（如PCN）如何在学习表示中平衡压缩与重构，并借助持续同调提供定量拓扑视角，弥补传统指标无法捕捉结构变化的不足。

Method: 在合成数据集和MNIST上训练高精度PCN，按层进行持续同调分析，计算连通分量等拓扑特征的演化，并通过Spearman相关、bootstrap比较不同架构、激活函数和模型宽度的影响。

Result: 小PCN更早压缩（Spearman ρ=0.72–0.79）；压缩深度与重构误差负相关（ρ=-0.58）；PCN比匹配MLP平均晚3.6层压缩连通分量。

Conclusion: 持续同调可有效量化PCN中的压缩-重构权衡，且该权衡的层间解析受模型容量和预测编码的递归双向推断动态共同影响。

Abstract: We study the topology of learned representations in predictive coding networks (PCNs), a neuro-inspired bidirectional architecture, using a quantitative layer-wise persistent homology analysis. We train well-performing PCNs on a synthetic classification dataset ($\geq 99.9\%$ test accuracy) and on MNIST ($\geq 95\%$ test accuracy), and measure how topological features change across layers for different architectures and activation functions. We find that smaller PCNs collapse connected components across layers earlier than larger models (Spearman $\unicode{x1D70C} \in [0.72, 0.79]$ across activations), with model size measured as the sum of hidden-layer widths. We also observe a strong negative correlation ($\unicode{x1D70C} = -0.58$) between the depth at which simplification occurs and reconstruction error; i.e., architectures that simplify later reconstruct better. Finally, a seed-level bootstrap comparison across architectures and activations shows that PCNs consistently collapse connected components later than matched MLPs, with an average difference of $3.6$ layers. These results suggest that persistent homology offers a useful quantitative lens on the compression--reconstruction tradeoff in PCNs, and that both model capacity and the recurrent, bidirectional dynamics of predictive coding inference shape when this tradeoff is resolved across layers.

</details>


### [20] [UrbanAgent: A Tool-Augmented Agent for Cross-System Urban Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03018)
*Jiayu Cao,Xingyuan Zeng,feiyu Li,Zhijing Huang,Xujie Yuan,Rongxiang Chen,Shimin Di,Libin Zheng,Jian Yin*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Urban-Agent框架，通过工具增强和自适应闭环，将大语言模型与代码执行、API调用、MCP协议结合，解决跨系统城市任务中的复杂自然语言请求到可执行工作流的转换问题，并引入Urban-Eval基准进行评测，任务成功率比最强基线高10个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字服务碎片化、互操作性差，平台、城市基础模型和智能助手仅解决孤立问题，难以可靠地将复杂自然语言请求转化为跨系统可执行工作流，且缺乏专门针对跨系统城市任务的评估基准。

Method: Urban-Agent结合大语言模型的认知推理能力与工具集（代码执行、API调用、模型上下文协议），通过自适应闭环在行动前澄清缺失信息、基于实时观测使用工具、使最终响应与观测证据和任务约束对齐；同时构建Urban-Eval基准，评估任务结果和执行质量（工具覆盖、依赖有效性和证据可追溯性）。

Result: Urban-Agent在任务成功率达71%，超过最强基线10个百分点，且在GPT-5-mini、Gemini-2.5-flash、DeepSeek-V4-flash和Qwen3-235B-A22B等不同模型上均保持优势。

Conclusion: Urban-Agent有效提升了跨系统城市任务的处理能力，Urban-Eval填补了该领域评测空白，证明了所提方法在鲁棒性和泛化性上的优势。

Abstract: Modern cities rely on an increasing number of digital services to operate, but residents' daily needs are still difficult to meet. Services are fragmented and have little interoperability, placing a heavy operational burden on users. Existing digital platforms, urban foundation models, and intelligent assistants each address only isolated aspects of an urban task. But they struggle to reliably convert complex natural-language requests into executable cross-system workflows. We propose Urban-Agent, a tool-augmented agent framework for cross-system urban tasks. It couples the cognitive and reasoning capabilities of a large language model with a tool-set supporting code execution, API calls, and Model Context Protocol. Through one adaptive closed loop, it clarifies missing information before acting, grounds tool use in live observations, and aligns the final response with observed evidence and task constraints. To address the evaluation gap, we introduce Urban-Eval, a benchmark specifically designed for cross-system urban request. Unlike prior benchmarks that assess either general tool use or urban knowledge and reasoning, Urban-Eval evaluates both task results and execution quality, including required tool coverage, dependency validity, and evidence traceability. Experimental results indicate that Urban-Agent reaches a 71% task success rate, 10 points above the strongest baseline. This lead holds across GPT-5-mini, Gemini-2.5-flash, DeepSeek-V4-flash, and Qwen3-235B-A22B.

</details>


### [21] [Wiring Beats Blending: What Transfers Between Transformer Sizes -- and What Doesn't](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02829)
*Ravi Satya Durga Prasad Yenugula*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 将预训练大模型转换为更小的同族模型是可行的，其价值在于初始化而非权重本身；通过最小二乘补偿和方差保持重缩放两个独立杠杆，在低预算下可实现高效、高质量的子模型初始化，但存在尺度上限和病态问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型族每个尺寸都从头训练，成本高昂；能否复用已训练的大模型，通过某种转换生成更小的同族模型，以节省训练资源并提升低预算下的下游性能。

Method: 以Pythia 1.4B->410M为核心，系统分析表示与参数的对齐性；提出最佳线性投影（最小二乘补偿）与方差保持重缩放两个独立操作，结合匹配预算的继续预训练验证其有效性；通过打乱对照和统计检验区分真信号与伪影；探索尺度扩大（6.9B->1.4B）时的失效边界并诊断病态原因。

Result: 表示对齐强（R^2=0.84）而参数对齐弱；直接权重投影破坏结构；最佳线性投影后残差近噪声；补偿在30M token下优于最强子克隆变体（84.0±1.8 vs 89.7±3.7，3/3种子），33倍预算下收敛持平（40.0 vs 40.0）；转移初始化低预算下领先最高18倍；6.9B->1.4B时双杠杆过校正，归因于补偿求解病态。

Conclusion: 模型转换的价值在初始化而非权重本身，补偿与重缩放为token高效的低预算工具，但仅适用于相近尺度；需维度感知正则化解决大宽度下的病态问题，为高效模型族构建提供新路径。

Abstract: Model families train every size from scratch. Can a pretrained large model be converted into a smaller sibling? We characterize the 1.4B->410M conversion in the Pythia family end-to-end: (i) representations align strongly across sizes (ridge R^2=0.84) while parameters align weakly; (ii) dense weight projection is functionally destructive -- provably not an assembly artifact -- because basis mixing breaks rotary, per-head, GELU, and LayerNorm structure; (iii) after the best-fit linear operator, weight residuals are statistically indistinguishable from noise under shuffle controls; (iv) conversion value therefore lives in initialization. In matched-budget continued pre-training we decompose conversion into two independent levers -- least-squares compensation (function: best zero-shot) and variance-preserving rescale (dynamics: best endpoints). Compensation is a token-efficient, low-budget win rather than a universal one: at 30M tokens it beats the strongest subcloning variant on both a width-reduced pair (84.0 +/- 1.8 vs. 89.7 +/- 3.7, 3/3 seeds) and a held-out depth-reduced pair (109.3 vs. 117.9, 3/3 seeds), reaching a given quality with fewer tokens; at a 33x larger budget the two converge to parity (40.0 vs. 40.0), both far ahead of from-scratch, which transfer initialization always beats -- by up to 18x at low budget, the margin narrowing at convergence and at the largest scale. We further map the method's boundary: at ~5x the donor scale (6.9B->1.4B) stacking both levers over-corrects, which we trace to ill-conditioning of the compensation solve at large width, pointing to dimension-aware regularization as the fix. Code, checkpoints, and the frozen evaluation corpus are released.

</details>


### [22] [LoCA: Forward-Only LLM Tuning after One-Shot Calibration with Local Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03020)
*Linhan Xia,Rui Liu,Zhaofeng Zhang,Yihao Wang,Binrui Shen,Shengxin Zhu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出LoCA方法，用一次校准替代重复反向传播，实现前向-only的微调，在降低内存和峰值的同时保持与LoRA相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调虽减少可训练参数，但仍需对冻结主干进行端到端反向传播，每次更新都依赖支持反向的硬件且需存储或重算激活值，成本高且不灵活。

Method: LoCA分两阶段：先通过一次探测反向传播在每个Transformer块拟合低秩映射，将最终预测误差映射为局部隐藏状态修正；随后利用这些映射从前向激活构造逐块回归目标，并以闭式岭回归拟合低秩适配器，不再需要主干反向传播。

Result: 在Qwen2.5 0.5B至14B的五个判别基准上，25项任务-规模比较中16项LoCA的评估交叉熵低于LoRA；峰值GPU内存降低26-29%，校准后CPU稳态内存降低36-52%，每遍时间降低43-48%。

Conclusion: LoCA将全局信用分配摊销到一次校准中，使得后续只需前向计算，适用于反复反向传播不可行的场景，且跨模型规模具有可迁移性。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient post-training reduces the number of trainable parameters, but still requires repeated end-to-end backpropagation through the frozen backbone. Every adaptation step therefore needs backward-capable hardware and must store or recompute activations. We ask whether this repeated backward chain can be replaced by a one-time calibration. We introduce Local Credit Assignment (LoCA), a two-stage method for small-shift adaptation. One probe backward pass fits a low-rank map at each transformer block from the final prediction error to a local hidden-state correction. LoCA then reuses these maps to form blockwise regression targets from forward activations and fits low-rank adapters with closed-form ridge solves. No further backbone backward pass is required. We evaluate LoCA on five discriminative benchmarks with Qwen2.5 models from 0.5B to 14B. In 16 of 25 reported task--scale comparisons, LoCA yields lower evaluation cross-entropy than the corresponding LoRA run. Its measured full-run GPU peak, including calibration, is 26--29\% lower than LoRA's. After calibration, its CPU steady-state memory is 36--52\% lower and its per-pass time is 43--48\% lower. A shared scale-normalized candidate set is reused across all tested Qwen2.5 sizes and on SmolLM2-1.7B. LoCA thus amortizes global credit assignment into one calibration and enables later forward-only tuning when repeated backpropagation is impractical. The code associated with this paper is available \href{https://github.com/Xia12121/LoCA}{here}.

</details>


### [23] [NOMADD: Numerical Optimization of Models Adapting to Data Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02845)
*Swapn Shah,Keith Burghardt*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种后置式概念漂移缓解方法，通过对各时间段的基模型参数进行低秩分解与外推预测，在18个数据集的基准上显著提升多种模型性能，训练仅需数秒，推理远快于现有SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据中数据漂移和概念漂移会降低模型性能，而现有缓解方法多为定制化的神经网络训练方案，难以应用于树模型或基础模型，且实时重训练不现实。需要一种通用、轻量、后置的漂移处理方法，以在模型精度、推理时间和模型大小等约束下灵活适配多种模型。

Method: 对每个带标签的训练时间段分别拟合基模型，以所有时间段池化后的模型作为锚点，测量各时段模型参数相对锚点的演变；对参数变化做低秩分解，压缩为若干潜在因子；对每个潜在因子用带阻尼的规则化方法进行前向外推，得到当前或未来时段的模型参数，从而在不重新训练的前提下减轻概念漂移。

Result: 在Drift-Resilient TabPFN基准的18个数据集上，按该基准的协议和指标评估，外推方法对所有基模型族均带来性能提升，效果与当前SOTA（Drift-Resilient TabPFN）相当，但训练仅需数秒（对比后者需预训练数百万合成数据集、约1300 GPU小时），且推理速度提升数个数量级。

Conclusion: 该方法作为通用后置工具，能以极低成本提升多种表格模型的漂移鲁棒性，在精度和效率上具有显著优势；未来值得探索将其扩展到其他模态，但也面临一定挑战。

Abstract: Tabular model performance degrades when feature distributions change over time or the relationship between features and outcome variables change over time, known as data drift and concept drift, respectively. These issues are challenging to mitigate in real time because labeled data may not be immediately available, or re-training a model could be impractical. While tools exist to reduce drift, they are typically bespoke to neural network architectures and adapt how models are trained. In this paper, we offer an alternative post-hoc method to reduce concept drift, which is applicable to a variety of models, from trees to neural networks to tabular foundation models. This new tool is especially useful when constraints, such as high model accuracy, bounded inference time, or model size requires users to choose between different models for their specific use-cases. Our algorithm fits the base model separately on each labeled training period, measures how its parameters evolve against a single anchor model pooled over all of those periods, compresses those changes with a low-rank factorization, and extrapolates each latent factor forward with a damped, regularized forecast. On the 18-dataset Drift-Resilient TabPFN benchmark, evaluated under that benchmark's own protocol and metric, the extrapolation improves every base family it is applied to, and achieves performance competitive with the state-of-the-art Drift-Resilient TabPFN with seconds of training. In contrast, Drift-Resilient TabPFN requires pre-training on millions of synthetic datasets over approximately 1,300 GPU-hours, and is orders of magnitude slower in inference (depending on the model). In the discussion, we explore the promise and challenges of extending this tool to other modalities.

</details>


### [24] [DiffImaginE: Imagine to Verify Entity Types with Diffusio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03025)
*Feng Zhang,Feiyu Han,Rongxin Yang,Yang Liu,Yancheng Chen,Rui Wang,Yingguang Yang,Tian Xueyun,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Zheng,Xu Kefu,Congjing Ran,Fuhai Chen,Bin Chong*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出 DiffImaginE，将多模态命名实体识别（MNER）的类型验证建模为条件潜扩散推理，用去噪误差作为类型条件负对数似然的 ELBO 一致替代，以对竞争类型假设进行排序，在 Twitter-2015 和 Twitter-2017 上优于匹配的确定性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 imagine-and-compare 验证器将每个（span, type）对压缩为单一预测视觉原型，无法捕捉多样视觉实现，且兼容性分数缺乏明确概率语义，难以进行严格的类型假设比较。

Method: 保留标准多模态编码器，用分类器无关引导的扩散评分器替换确定性验证器；直接监督每类型扩散分数作为分类 logits，学习跨噪声水平的聚合，并使用对偶抽样降低蒙特卡洛比较方差；训练采用 Min-SNR 加权。

Result: 在 Twitter-2015 和 Twitter-2017 上，与同编码器、辅助目标和评估协议的确定性 ImaginE 对照相比取得一致提升，消融和配对显著性检验支持结果。

Conclusion: DiffImaginE 表明条件潜扩散推理能有效替代确定性验证器，提供更优的类型区分能力和概率解释，并揭示引导强度与对偶抽样的理论性质，为多模态验证任务提供新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal named entity recognition (MNER) determines whether each candidate span and entity-type hypothesis is supported by joint textual and visual evidence. Existing imagine-and-compare verifiers map each (span, type) pair to one predicted visual feature, compressing diverse visual realisations into a single prototype and providing a compatibility score without explicit probabilistic semantics. We introduce DiffImaginE, which formulates MNER type verification as conditional latent diffusion inference. Given span-localised visual evidence, a type-conditioned denoiser predicts noise injected into its standardised latent. The resulting denoising error provides an ELBO-consistent surrogate for type-conditional negative log-likelihood, allowing competing type hypotheses to be ranked by how well they explain the observation. DiffImaginE retains a standard multimodal encoder stack and replaces the deterministic verifier with a classifier-free-guided diffusion scorer trained using Min-SNR weighting. We directly supervise per-type diffusion scores as classification logits, learn aggregation across noise levels, and use antithetic sampling to reduce Monte Carlo comparison variance. Our analysis shows that classifier-free guidance sharpens the induced type posterior and characterises when antithetic pairing reduces variance at equal denoiser cost. Experiments on Twitter-2015 and Twitter-2017 show consistent gains over a matched deterministic ImaginE control under the same encoder, auxiliary objectives, and evaluation protocol, supported by ablations and paired significance tests.

</details>


### [25] [CastFSR: A Fast--Slow--Reflect Agentic Reasoning Framework for Context-Aware Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03031)
*Xiaoyu Tao,Mingyue Cheng,Bokai Pan,Chuang Jiang,Huanjian Zhang,Tian Gao,Yaguo Liu,Qi Liu,Enhong Chen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出CastFSR，一个将上下文感知预测建模为快-慢-反思工作流的智能体框架，显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的时序预测方法缺乏显式机制来识别相关上下文、推理其影响，并基于时间与领域约束验证预测。

Method: 采用快思考生成数据驱动预测先验，慢思考检索上下文证据并推理其影响，反思阶段迭代修正预测以满足约束；支持免训练推理及通过两阶段SFT+RL将编排能力迁移至轻量LLM。

Result: 在公共数据集上，CastFSR一致优于代表性基线。

Conclusion: CastFSR有效整合上下文推理与预测验证，兼顾性能与部署效率，代码已开源。

Abstract: Time series forecasting is fundamental to decision-making in complex systems, where future dynamics are influenced not only by historical observations but also by evolving contextual features. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have extended forecasting beyond numerical extrapolation toward context-aware reasoning. However, existing approaches often lack explicit mechanisms to identify relevant contexts, reason about their impacts, and validate forecasts against temporal and domain constraints. In this work, we propose CastFSR, an agentic framework that formulates context-aware forecasting as a Fast--Slow--Reflect workflow. In fast thinking, CastFSR profiles observations and selects lightweight forecasters to construct a data-driven forecast prior. In slow deliberation, it retrieves contextual evidence, adaptively determines informative look-back windows, and reasons about how contexts reshape future dynamics. In reflection, it iteratively refines forecasts to ensure temporal, contextual, and domain consistency. CastFSR supports both training-free inference with off-the-shelf LLMs and efficient deployment through a two-stage SFT and reinforcement learning strategy that transfers its orchestration capability to compact LLMs. Extensive experiments on public datasets demonstrate that CastFSR consistently outperforms representative baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/Xiaoyu-Tao/CastFSR.

</details>


### [26] [Maglev: Sliding Recurrent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02870)
*Bo Liu,Qiang Liu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种固定大小记忆的循环Transformer架构，通过双模型耦合和记忆一致性损失，在保持训练并行性的同时提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有滑动窗口注意力缺乏长程依赖建模能力，潜在循环Transformer训练难以并行化；需要一种兼顾并行训练与高效推理的架构。

Method: 构造预填充器Q（使用全注意力）生成记忆目标m'_t，解码器P（仅滑动窗口+循环K/V注入）生成用于预测的记忆m_t；训练时用一致性损失对齐两者，推理时仅用P；可对P、Q参数共享以节省内存。

Result: 相比滑动窗口注意力和潜在循环Transformer基线，在验证损失和下游预训练基准上均获得改进；参数共享能在保留大部分收益的同时减少参数占用。

Conclusion: 该架构有效结合了全注意力表达力与循环记忆效率，为长序列建模提供实用方案，并支持灵活的参数-性能权衡。

Abstract: We introduce \ours{}, a recurrent Transformer architecture with fixed-size memory that generalizes sliding-window attention while remaining parallelizable during training. \ours{} consists of two coupled models: a prefiller $Q$, which leverages full attention\footnote{In practice, we use interleaved full and sliding-window attention for $Q$, as this yields stronger performance. The essential requirement is that $Q$ be more expressive than $P$, with access to the full history.} to produce memory targets $m'_t$, and a decoder $P$, which uses only sliding-window attention and recurrent K/V injection to produce decoder memories $m_t$ for next-token prediction. We train \ours{} with a memory consistency loss that aligns $m_t$ with $m'_t$, allowing inference to use $P$ alone. Empirically, \ours{} improves validation loss and downstream pretraining benchmarks over sliding-window and latent recurrent transformer baselines. Moreover, sharing parameters between $P$ and $Q$ reduces parameter memory while preserving most of the gains.

</details>


### [27] [TraceCAD: Trace-Guided Repair for Agentic CAD Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03062)
*Fengxiao Fan,Jingzhe Ni,Fan Sang,Xiaolong Yin,Yu Liu,Ruofeng Tong,Min Tang,Peng Du*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出TraceCAD，一种为LLM驱动的CAD智能体设计的恢复层，通过持久化状态链接需求、建模步骤和失败证据，实现局部搜索和技能复用，提升修复质量与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的CAD程序在修正循环中会丢失关于已满足需求、错误操作和先前修复的证据，导致修复效率低且质量不稳定。

Method: TraceCAD将请求特征、建模步骤、失败证据和候选结果作为持久状态保存；诊断可能出错的操作，在其依赖区域内进行有界编辑搜索，通过执行与保留检查验证候选，并将成功/失败修复存入可复用技能记忆。

Result: 在DeepCAD基准上（200模型消融和1K模型对比），IoU、Chamfer距离和Hausdorff距离达到竞争性几何质量；去除持久状态使恢复分数减半，去除局部搜索使几何回归翻倍并增加代码智能体调用；预热技能库可减少重试、令牌成本和延迟。

Conclusion: 持久化、局部化且可复用的恢复机制能显著提升最终CAD质量和修复可靠性。

Abstract: LLM-based CAD agents produce executable parametric programs, but their correction loops may lose evidence about satisfied requirements, faulty operations, and prior repairs. We introduce TraceCAD, a recovery layer that links requested features, modeling steps, failure evidence, and candidate outcomes as persistent state. TraceCAD diagnoses likely faulty operations, searches bounded edits in their dependency regions, validates candidates through execution and preservation checks, and retains successful and failed repair outcomes in reusable skill memory. On DeepCAD-derived benchmarks with 200-model ablations and a 1K-model comparison, TraceCAD achieves competitive geometric quality in terms of IoU, Chamfer distance, and Hausdorff distance. Removing persistent state nearly halves recovery score; removing localized search more than doubles geometric regression and doubles code-agent invocations. Initializing the skill store on disjoint training models further reduces retries, token cost, and latency. These results demonstrate that persistent, localized, and reusable recovery improves final CAD quality and repair reliability.

</details>


### [28] [GoT-CD: Graph-of-Thoughts Causal Discovery and the Fragility of Post-hoc Path-Specific Fairness Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02877)
*Nitish Nagesh,Elahe Khatibi,Thomas Dean Hughes,Mahdi Bagheri,Pratik Gajane,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出GoT-CD方法，用完整边集作为推理单元，通过并行生成、确定性评分和硬约束合并来发现因果图，在保持结构竞争力的同时，发现结构保真度不足以保证路径特定公平性审计的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法在结构指标上表现良好，但未评估特定路径对公平性审计的影响；结构误差可能导致审计报告失真（如路径缺失时误报零效应）。

Method: GoT-CD将推理单元设为完整候选边集，并行生成多个图，用确定性有效性函数评分，在禁止发明边的硬并约束下合并，并通过贪婪投影强制生成DAG。

Result: 在五个基准上均返回有效DAG，在Asia、Alzheimer's和COVID-Respiratory数据集上取得LLM方法中最佳DAG有效F1分数；在Alzheimer's基准上，8个发现图中5个无法恢复敏感属性到结局的路径，导致总体效应为零而中介效应持续存在。

Conclusion: 结构发现与路径特定公平性分析必须结合，仅凭结构保真度不足以支持公平性审计，需在下游进行路径级检查。

Abstract: Causal discovery recovers directed structure from observational data and is increasingly used in clinical settings to support mechanism reasoning and fairness audits of predictive models. Path-specific counterfactual fairness asks whether a protected attribute influences an outcome through illegitimate pathways, but these estimands are defined relative to a supplied causal graph and therefore inherit whatever errors the discovery step introduces. Discovery methods are routinely scored on aggregate structural metrics that weight all edges equally, and no established evaluation asks whether the specific pathway an audit depends on survives discovery---or what the audit reports when that pathway is missing. Here we show that full-graph Graph-of-Thoughts reasoning yields acyclic discovered graphs that are structurally competitive with large language model (LLM) baselines, yet that structural fidelity alone does not guarantee fairness-faithful audits. We introduce GoT-CD, in which the reasoning unit is a complete candidate edge set: multiple graphs are generated in parallel, scored by a deterministic validity function, and merged under a hard union constraint that forbids invented edges, with greedy projection enforcing a DAG before commitment. GoT-CD returns a valid DAG on all five reported benchmarks and achieves the best DAG-valid F1 score among LLM methods on Asia, Alzheimer's, and COVID-Respiratory datasets. On an Alzheimer's benchmark with known unfair path, a post-hoc path-specific audit shows that five of eight discovered graphs recover no path from the sensitive attribute to the outcome and therefore report a null overall effect while mediated effects persist, necessitating downstream path-specific fairness analysis along with structural discovery.

</details>


### [29] [Getting the Parameters Right: A Difficulty-Graded Benchmark and Probe-Guided Training for LLM Tool Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03071)
*Guoyao Yu,Xiaoqing Sun,Ziqi Huang,Shaojing Fan,Zhongyi Zhang,Xiaomeng Hu,Xiaobo Xue,Yangyang Shi,Xiong Xiao,Yang Song,Biao Lyu,Rong Wen,Xing Li,Qinming He,Shunming Zhu,Zhenguang Liu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出基于隐状态线性探针的统一框架（PBT和PGR），显著提升LLM智能体工具调用参数生成的正确率，在ParamBench上平均精确匹配率从19.7%提升至59.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用研究侧重工具选择和调用顺序，忽略参数填充正确性；在云网络等专业领域，前沿模型参数调用正确率不足一半。

Method: 首先发现模型生成参数值时隐状态包含正确性信号，可用线性探针预测；据此提出探针过滤自举训练（PBT）用于微调数据筛选，以及探针引导重排（PGR）用于推理时候选选择；并构建ParamBench基准，按参数嵌套深度、跨参数依赖和推理需求划分五级难度。

Result: 在5个开源模型、ParamBench及6个外部基准上，参数生成平均精确匹配率从19.7%提升至59.6%，验证了方法的普适性和有效性。

Conclusion: 探针引导框架能有效利用隐状态正确性信号，低成本提升工具参数生成能力，为复杂API调用提供可行方案，并释放ParamBench支持后续研究。

Abstract: Large language model agents derive much of their capability from tool use. Existing research on tool use has largely focused on selecting the right tool and orchestrating the order of calls. However, correctly filling the parameters of a tool call is equally critical for successful execution and has received far less attention. In domains such as cloud networking, even frontier models correctly complete fewer than half of tool calls. Inspired by recent analyses showing that LLM hidden states encode rich information about model predictions, we discover that while the model generates a parameter value, its hidden state contains a strong correctness signal: a simple linear probe can accurately predict whether the value will be correct. Based on this observation, we propose a unified probe-guided framework with two complementary approaches: probe-filtered bootstrapped training (PBT), which uses the probe to filter reliable self-generated calls for fine-tuning, and probe-guided reranking (PGR), which uses the probe to select better candidates during inference. To support systematic evaluation, we release ParamBench, a benchmark built from real cloud-network APIs that categorizes every instance into five difficulty levels according to parameter nesting depth, cross-parameter dependencies, and the reasoning required to derive values from earlier calls. Extensive experiments across 5 open models on ParamBench and 6 external benchmarks demonstrate that our method substantially improves parameter generation, raising the average exact match from 19.7% to 59.6%.

</details>


### [30] [Population-Robust Feature Selection via Generalized Welfare Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02887)
*Ruiqi Lyu,Alistair Turcan,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出PopFS方法，在多个异质群体中学习一个共享且稳健的特征集，同时允许各群体训练自己的模型，通过可调福利目标平衡整体收益与最差群体保护，并在大规模数据和COVID-19案例中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准特征选择方法通常针对单一大规模群体优化，而现有稳健方法则对所有群体学习共享模型，忽略了群体异质性。实际部署中，有限的问卷或传感器需服务多个不同群体，因此需要一种既能共享特征集又能适应群体差异的方法。

Method: PopFS使用可调的福利目标函数，允许实践者平衡整体预测效益和对收益最少群体的保护。为提升可扩展性，先通过多任务稀疏学习缩减候选特征池，再直接搜索硬特征集，通过排序有希望的添加和交换操作，仅对短名单进行完整重拟合。

Result: 在来自六个预测任务的八个人口划分（覆盖五个表格和公共卫生数据集）中，PopFS一致取得优秀的平均性能和最差群体性能，且可扩展到数千候选特征。43州COVID-19现在预测研究显示，调整福利目标能以较小平均性能代价改善最差州的表现，并产生可解释的症状信号变化。

Conclusion: PopFS为异质群体下的共享特征选择提供了有效且可扩展的解决方案，兼顾整体性能与公平性，并具有实际部署价值。代码已公开。

Abstract: Choosing which features to collect is a deployment decision: the same limited questionnaire, test panel, or sensor set may need to serve several heterogeneous populations. Standard feature-selection methods typically optimize for one large population, while existing robust approaches tend to learn one shared model for every population. We introduce PopFS, a method for learning one shared, deployable feature set that is robust to population differences while letting each pop- ulation train its own model. PopFS uses a tunable welfare objective that lets practitioners balance overall predictive ben- efit against stronger protection of the populations that benefit least. To make this objective practical at scale, PopFS first uses multitask sparse learning to reduce the candidate pool, then searches directly over hard feature sets by ranking promising additions and swaps and fully refitting only a shortlist. Across eight population splits from six prediction tasks drawn from five tabular and public-health datasets, PopFS consistently achieves strong average and worst-population performance while scaling to thousands of candidate features. A 43-state COVID-19 nowcasting study further shows that changing the welfare objective can improve the least-served states with lit- tle change in average performance and yields an interpretable change in the selected symptom signals. Our code is available at https://github.com/Rachel-Lyu/PopFS.

</details>


### [31] [Robust Counterfactual Policy Optimisation via Nondeterministic Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02893)
*Jessica Lally,Milad Kazemi,Nicola Paoletti,David Watson,Sander Beckers*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 首次在概率非确定性因果模型下形式化反事实策略优化，提出敏感分析框架下的鲁棒反事实策略求解方法，并在败血症模拟器上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实推断假设确定性因果模型，无法处理MDP内在随机性；需区分潜在混杂与不可约随机性，但缺乏相应的反事实策略优化框架。

Method: 提出概率非确定性因果模型，将潜在混杂与随机性分离；基于敏感分析构建鲁棒反事实策略优化问题，并给出首个实用性求解方案。

Result: 在败血症治疗模拟器中（糖尿病作为隐藏全局混杂），方法成功识别出鲁棒反事实策略，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为随机MDP下的反事实策略优化提供了理论建模和实用算法，有助于在混杂存在下做出稳健的序贯决策。

Abstract: Counterfactual inference approaches for sequential decision-making typically assume deterministic causal models, where all randomness stems from latent variables. However, Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) are inherently stochastic. We address this by formalising counterfactual policy optimisation under probabilistic nondeterministic causal models, which properly separates latent confounding from irreducible stochasticity, and here propose a first practical optimisation problem for identifying robust counterfactual policies under a sensitivity analysis framework. We validate our approach on a sepsis treatment simulator, where diabetes status acts as a hidden global confounder.

</details>


### [32] [Don't Peek at the Answer: Outcome-Masked Group Relative Policy Optimization for Label-Free RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03119)
*Yongshi Ye,Liang Zhang,Yidong Chen,Xiaodong Shi,Biao Fu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出OM-GRPO框架，通过掩码答案跨度梯度与对比增强奖励，解决无标签RLVR中基于答案共识奖励导致的奖励估计与策略优化耦合崩溃问题，性能媲美有监督GT训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR依赖GT答案限制扩展性；基于投票的无标签RLVR虽免除人工标注，但将同一答案级信号同时用于奖励估计与token级策略优化，导致模型直接强化答案token而非改善推理，引发训练崩溃。

Method: OM-GRPO解耦奖励估计与策略优化：1）对答案跨度梯度掩码，保留软共识奖励信号，将优化压力从答案token转移；2）引入对比增强奖励，利用已有轨迹的廉价两两比较细化奖励估计，无需额外采样。

Result: 在多个推理基准和三种LLM骨干下，OM-GRPO一致优于现有无标签RLVR方法，并与有监督GT奖励训练持平；在测试时训练场景中，比多数投票提升4.24个点。

Conclusion: OM-GRPO提供稳定无标签RLVR训练方案，有效避免崩溃，且具备与有监督方法相当的推理性能，尤其适用于测试时训练等场景。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) improves LLM reasoning but typically relies on ground-truth (GT) answers, limiting scalability. Voting-based label-free RLVR replace gold supervision with answer-level consensus from model samples. However, collapse arises when the same answer-level signal is used both to estimate rewards and to drive token-level policy optimization, encouraging the model to directly reinforce answer tokens rather than improve reasoning. We propose OM-GRPO, a label-free RLVR framework that decouples reward estimation from policy optimization. OM-GRPO masks gradients on the answer span while retaining answer-level rewards through a soft consensus signal, shifting optimization pressure away from answer tokens. We further introduce Contrast-Augmented Reward, which refines reward estimation via low-cost pairwise comparisons over existing trajectories without additional rollouts. Across diverse reasoning benchmarks and three LLM backbones, OM-GRPO consistently outperforms existing label-free RLVR methods and matches supervised GT-reward training with stable optimization. This stability is particularly beneficial in the Test-Time Training setting, where OM-GRPO surpasses majority voting by 4.24 points.

</details>


### [33] [Verifiable Memory: Learning Unified Memory Management with Local and Global Verifiers for Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03137)
*Xiaolong Sun,Qichao Wang,Hangyu Li,Liang Chen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出VerMem框架，用统一策略控制长期记忆、活动上下文和情节历史，通过七种原子操作和三级强化学习课程训练，在多个基准上取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常分别优化长期记忆和短期记忆，且统一策略多依赖轨迹级反馈，难以对单个记忆决策提供精确信用分配。

Method: 将长期记忆、活动上下文和情节历史建模为不同状态，用一个策略控制七种原子操作；通过监督微调初始化，再经三阶段强化学习课程训练，结合局部验证器、全局验证器及分层信用分配。

Result: 在五个基准和两个LLM骨干上，大部分指标达到最优，并在受控在线token预算下取得最强效率-性能前沿。

Conclusion: 验证器仅用于训练，表明该方法可有效提升长时交互中的记忆管理性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents must retain reusable information, control a bounded active context, and recover earlier evidence during long-horizon interaction. Existing methods commonly optimize long-term memory (LTM) and short-term memory (STM) separately, while unified policies are often trained primarily with trajectory-level feedback, which provides weak credit for individual memory decisions. We present Verifiable Memory (VerMem), a framework that represents LTM, active context, and episodic history as distinct states and controls them with one memory operation policy. Seven atomic operations let the policy add, revise, or soft-delete LTM entries; retrieve LTM into the active context; filter or summarize the active context; and restore selected episodic fragments. VerMem is initialized by supervised fine-tuning and trained with a three-stage reinforcement-learning curriculum. The local verifier scores executable memory transitions, and a global verifier assesses evidence coherence and terminal-memory consistency after task completion. These scores are combined with programmatically computed task, evidence-recall, efficiency, and constraint signals through hierarchical credit assignment. The verifiers are used only during training. Across five benchmarks and two LLM backbones, VerMem achieves the best result on the vast majority of reported metrics and consistently outperforms strong memory baselines. Under controlled online-token budgets on three interactive benchmarks, it also achieves the strongest efficiency--performance frontier among the compared methods. Code is available at https://github.com/Sun-SYSU-24/VerMem.

</details>


### [34] [Forecasting Revenue with its Customer-Base Drivers: When and Why Coordination Helps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02911)
*Kyeongbin Kim,Daniel McCarthy,Dokyun Lee*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出CBMT多任务Transformer模型，联合预测客户群原始指标（获客、复购、客单价）并聚合为总收入，误差比最强基准低30%，比直接预测总收入的Transformer低2.65%（不显著），在74.3%企业中优于单任务模型，且在高波动场景下优势收窄。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 总收入预测无法揭示增长来源（获客、复购、每单花费或相互抵消），而分别预测客户群原始指标并保持一致性，能更好地支持预算分配、需求规划和基于客户的估值。

Method: 利用966家公司、25个行业的周度交易面板，构建客户基多任务Transformer（CBMT），共享底层结构、保留各原始指标的独立预测（获客、复购、支出），并通过对齐机制将原始预测组合以拟合下游总收入。

Result: CBMT的总销售额平均绝对误差比最强客户基基准低30%；比直接预测总收入的Transformer低2.65%（配对差异p=.222，不显著）；在74.3%的公司中优于单独估计的单任务模型；在24个基准-结果比较中有23个MAE更低，其余差异统计上不显著。

Conclusion: 协调的客户基预测能显著改善收入规划，当原始指标协同变动较强时联合预测更有效；但在客户群动态高度波动时所有模型精度下降且CBMT优势缩小，路由规则无法稳定优于始终使用CBMT，因此应用时应视波动性谨慎部署。

Abstract: Revenue forecasts guide acquisition budgets, demand planning, and customer-based valuations, yet an aggregate forecast does not show whether change reflects acquisition, repeat purchasing, spending per order, or offsetting movements. Using weekly transaction panels for 966 companies in 25 industries, the authors develop the Customer-Based Multi-task Transformer (CBMT), which learns shared structure, retains separate primitive forecasts, and aligns their combination with downstream revenue. CBMT's mean total-sales error is 30% below the strongest representative established customer-base benchmark. It is also 2.65% below a Transformer that forecasts total sales directly, although the paired difference is not statistically significant (p=.222), and it beats separately estimated single-task forecasts for 74.3% of firms. CBMT's source MAE is lower in 23 of 24 benchmark-by-outcome comparisons, with the remaining difference not statistically distinguishable from zero. Firms whose primitives co-move more strongly are more likely to benefit from joint forecasting; selected-family scenario-3 comparisons are consistent with gains from shared representation and revenue alignment but remain diagnostic rather than causal. Accuracy deteriorates for all models when customer-base dynamics are highly volatile, and CBMT's advantage narrows there. Calibration-period routing rules do not improve average accuracy over always deploying CBMT. The results show how coordinated customer-base forecasts support revenue planning and when they warrant greater caution.

</details>


### [35] [UniGD: A Unified Generative-Discriminative Framework for Industrial Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03150)
*Shujie Ji,Yawei Kong,Yilin Zhao,Li Wang,Xialong Liu,Peng Jiang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出统一生成-判别框架UniGD，将生成式检索与相关性评分融合于单模型，通过冲突感知梯度增强、码本锚定表示和异构广告素材建模，在工业搜索广告中实现收入提升5.78%、延迟降低33%，并在公开数据集上显著提升召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式检索系统将检索与独立的相关性模型级联，导致生成似然目标与查询-广告相关性判别解耦，损害效果且增加服务成本；同时多模态异构广告素材（短视频、商品、直播）缺乏统一语义建模，亟需一体化架构兼顾精度与效率。

Method: UniGD包含三部分：1) 冲突感知梯度增强(CAGE)自适应协调生成与判别目标的联合优化，缓解梯度干扰；2) 码本锚定表示模块(CAM)将物品表示锚定到从多模态预训练模型蒸馏的冻结分层码本，注入强语义先验；3) 异构广告素材建模(HAM)在共享骨干上捕获跨类型语义共性，同时保留类型专属建模能力。

Result: 快手搜索广告在线A/B测试中，广告收入提升5.78%，推理延迟降低33%，相关性判别估计改善；在NQ320K和MS300K上，Recall@10较最强复现GR基线分别提升8.44%和3.19%。

Conclusion: UniGD证明统一生成-判别单模型架构在工业级搜索广告中可行，兼顾效果、延迟与异构素材兼容性，为生成式检索落地提供有效方案。

Abstract: Generative retrieval (GR) is a promising paradigm for industrial search advertising, yet its deployment is constrained by strict relevance and latency requirements. Existing systems cascade GR with an independent relevance model, decoupling the generative likelihood objective from query-ad relevance discrimination, which compromises effectiveness and increases serving costs. We propose a Unified Generative-Discriminative framework (UniGD) that integrates retrieval and relevance scoring within a single model. To mitigate gradient interference in joint optimization, UniGD introduces Conflict-Aware Gradient Enhancement (CAGE) to adaptively coordinate the two objectives. UniGD further designs a Codebook-Anchored Representation Module (CAM) that anchors item representations to frozen hierarchical codebooks distilled from a multimodal pretrained model, thereby endowing them with rich and generalizable semantic priors. For heterogeneous short-video, product, and live-stream ads, UniGD proposes Heterogeneous Ad-material Modeling (HAM), which captures cross-type semantic commonality over a shared backbone while preserving type-specific modeling capacity. Online AB tests on Kuaishou search advertising platform show that UniGD raises ad revenue by 5.78%, reduces inference latency by 33%, and improves discriminative relevance estimation. ### [39] [ATFlash: Per-RoPE-Wavelength Attention Windows for Compute/Memory-Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02947)
*Shun-ichiro Hayashi,Daichi Mukunoki,Tetsuya Hoshino,Takahiro Katagiri*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种基于RoPE波长比例的查询-键距离窗口剪枝方法，可在保持注意力质量的同时减少37-48%的查询-键内积计算，并已集成到FlashAttention-4和FlashInfer中，实现最高1.31倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE注意力分数可分解为多个2D旋转频率对之和，每个频率对的波长决定了其位置区分范围。传统滑动窗口会完全屏蔽远距离键，而动态稀疏方法如MInference虽能保留关键项但开销大且依赖输入。本文旨在利用RoPE的结构特性，设计一种输入无关、可预测剪枝率的静态窗口，同时保留低频分量对远距离键的可达性。

Method: 定义每个RoPE频率对对应的波长，并为每个查询-键对设置距离阈值（与波长成正比）。剪枝时仅保留距离小于该阈值的频率项，所有键仍可通过低频分量被访问。剪枝率有闭式解（与序列长度N成对数关系），且不依赖输入。该方法与令牌级稀疏方法正交，可叠加使用。实现上通过裁剪查询-键收缩轴的部分项，不改变在线softmax递归逻辑，并最小化改动集成到FlashAttention-4预填充和FlashInfer解码中。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-0.5B和Llama-3.2-3B上，剪枝37-48%的内积项，top-1匹配率保持96-98%，平均输出分布KL散度为10^-3 nat级。在RULER、OpenAI-MRCR、LongCodeQA和∞Bench等长上下文基准上绝对分数基本不变。在RTX PRO 6000上，Llama端到端加速最高1.29倍（128K上下文）；Qwen2.5-7B-1M在剪枝57%内积项时，1M上下文加速1.31倍。

Conclusion: 该频率感知的静态窗口提供了一种高效且无损的注意力稀疏化方案，兼顾了理论可预测性与实际硬件加速，并可无缝集成到现有注意力实现中，为超长上下文场景提供了实用优化。

Abstract: The attention score with rotary position embeddings (RoPE) decomposes exactly into a sum over its 2D-rotation frequency pairs, and each pair's wavelength limits how far it can discriminate position. Aligned with this structure, we propose the per-RoPE-wavelength distance window: it prunes the query--key inner-product terms beyond a wavelength-proportional distance. Unlike a sliding window, every key remains reachable, at least through the low-frequency pairs. The reduction rate is input-independent, with a closed form logarithmic in the sequence length $N$, in contrast to dynamic-sparse methods like MInference. Such token-level selection is orthogonal to our frequency-level pruning. The window can therefore be applied on top of those methods. On Qwen2.5-0.5B and Llama-3.2-3B, the window prunes 37--48\% of the query--key inner-product terms within each model's native context length. </details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Structure-preserving de-identification replaces protected health information (PHI) with realistic same-type surrogates -- "Anna S." becomes "Maria S.", not [NAME] -- so that clinical text stays fluent and downstream tools keep working. We ask a narrow, testable question: on the spans a de-identifier actually masks, can downstream PHI detectors still find the surrogate? We introduce a paired, multi-detector evaluation protocol that (i) scores utility only on masked spans, decoupling coverage from utility; (ii) uses equivalence testing (TOST) rather than null-hypothesis significance testing, which is uninformative at our sample size (57k paired spans); and (iii) builds a surrogate-failure typology separating fixable generator defects from intrinsic detector limits. Across 11 detectors, 7 benchmarks, and 7 languages (1,750 documents), recall on masked spans moves from 76.1% to 74.9% -- a change our equivalence test shows is statistically equivalent to zero within a +/-2-point margin (p ~ 3e-9), with detector ranking preserved. *Max Torop,Aria Masoomi,Jennifer Dy*

TL;DR: 发现并表征了倒置引导向量（ISV）现象，即高判别性的引导向量沿正方向推动反而抑制目标概念，并提出无需生成的ISV判别方法，通过定向符号翻转改进现有引导流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有引导向量假设沿正方向移动可增强概念表达，但存在高度判别向量表现出相反效果的异常情况，破坏该假设的可靠性。

Conclusion: 系统性地揭示了ISV的存在与机理，并提供了一种低成本、无需生成的修正策略，显著增强了基于检测的引导向量在实际模型上的有效性。

A key assumption underpinning SVs is that they are linearly discriminative with respect to the concept: representations of texts that exhibit the concept are more aligned with the SV than those that do not, motivating shifts along the positive or negative SV direction to respectively promote or suppress the concept. In this work, we identify an inverted detection-control phenomenon in which some highly discriminative SVs that are aligned with positive representations can consistently promote the opposite behavior. We refer to such vectors as inverted-steering vectors (ISVs).
