These findings highlight chart annotation generation as a challenging task requiring semantic grounding and effective annotation design. Code and data will be released in a future version.

</details>


### [257] [Tight Worst-Case Bounds for the Smallest Eigenvalue of ReLU NTK Gram Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03368)
*Zhao Song*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文针对单位向量集合的连续ReLU导数Gram矩阵H，证明了其最小特征值满足与投影分离度Δ±和维度无关的下界Ω(Δ±/√log n)，并构造了达到匹配上界O(Δ±/√log n)的最坏情形，表明该界在常数意义下最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReLU导数Gram矩阵在神经网络优化和特征学习中具有核心作用，其最小特征值控制着梯度下降的收敛速度与泛化性能。理解该矩阵的谱性质有助于揭示高维空间中数据几何结构对训练动态的影响，并推动维度无关的收敛理论发展。

Method: 利用标准高斯方向上的平均gated内积构造H，引入投影分离度Δ±刻画向量对的几何间隔。证明下界时采用概率与覆盖数技术结合谱范数扰动分析；证明上界时通过构造特定向量构型，使Gram矩阵的最小特征值达到理论下限。

Result: 推导出λ_min(H)=Ω(Δ±/√log n)的通用下界，同时构造示例证明λ_min(H)=O(Δ±/√log n)，两者仅差常数因子，严格建立了该量的量级。

Conclusion: 连续ReLU导数Gram矩阵的最小特征值本质上由数据集的投影分离度决定，并受维度无关的对数因子控制。该结果补全了该矩阵谱下界的紧性刻画，为理解神经网络表示学习中的梯度传播提供了严谨的几何依据。

Abstract: For $n$ unit vectors $x_1,\ldots,x_n \in \mathbb{R}^d$, we study the continuous ReLU derivative Gram matrix $H$, whose entries are obtained by averaging pairwise gated inner products over a standard Gaussian direction. Writing $ Δ_\pm := \min_{i \neq j} \min\{ \|x_i-x_j\|_2, \|x_i+x_j\|_2 \} $ for their projective separation, we prove the universal dimension-free lower bound $ λ_{\min}(H) = Ω( Δ_\pm/\sqrt{\log n} ) $. Conversely, we construct worst-case families satisfying the matching upper bound $ λ_{\min}(H) = O( Δ_\pm/\sqrt{\log n} ) $, showing that this rate is tight up to universal constants.

</details>


### [258] [Reversing Arrows in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03512)
*Sefika Efeoglu,Adrian Paschke*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 首次系统研究LLM对逆关系方向性语义的建模能力，构建包含27种逆关系标签、5457个实例的基准，发现LLM存在系统性不对称，且关系描述和实体表示变化影响性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对LLM是否准确建模逆关系方向性（如mother vs child）的考察，该问题对知识图谱构建和语义理解至关重要。

Method: 构建逆关系方向性基准，采用多项选择提示框架评估五个开源LLM，并通过替换为合成/掩码实体检验关系描述和实体表示的敏感性。

Result: LLM在逆关系分类中表现出系统性不对称；关系描述并未一致提升性能；性能对实体表示变化敏感。

Conclusion: 当前LLM对逆关系方向性的建模存在明显缺陷，需要更关注语义方向性训练和评估方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance on text-to-knowledge graph generation and related tasks. Nevertheless, it is still unclear whether they accurately model the direction-dependent semantics of inverse relations, in which reversing the order of the arguments alters the meaning of a relation (e.g., \textit{mother} versus \textit{child}). To the best of our knowledge, this work presents the first systematic study of inverse relation directionality in LLMs, using a benchmark consisting of 5,457 instances spanning 27 distinct inverse relation labels. We evaluate five open-source LLMs under a multiple-choice prompting framework and further examine the influence of relation descriptions and entity representations by substituting the original entities with synthetic and masked entities. Our findings reveal systematic asymmetries in inverse relation classification across LLMs, indicate that relation descriptions do not consistently improve performance, and show that model performance can be sensitive to variations in entity representations.

</details>


### [259] [Stop Replacing Noise with Noise: Two-Source Reliability Assessment for Label Correction and Sample Reweighting in Label-Noise Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03432)
*Wenxiao Fan,Kan Li*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE框架，通过分别评估观测标签和伪标签的可靠性，避免传统方法中两者互补假设带来的噪声传播问题，在合成和真实噪声基准上提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重装修复方法使用单一清洁度分数同时控制观测标签和伪标签，导致两者可靠性互补耦合——降低对观测标签的信任会自动提高对伪标签的信任，这可能用另一个不可靠信号替代原有噪声信号，因为从损坏监督中学习的伪标签可能重复其试图纠正的噪声。

Method: TRACE框架采用双源可靠性评估：对观测标签使用损失拟合、浅层关系稳定性和预测一致性三个指标，对伪标签单独使用模型置信度，两个来源的分数分别控制目标修正和监督强度，避免互补性假设。

Result: 在合成和真实噪声基准上，TRACE改进了代表性重装修复基线，并产生了更可靠的伪监督信号。

Conclusion: 通过解耦观测标签和伪标签的可靠性评估，可以更有效地进行标签修正和样本重加权，从而提升噪声标签学习的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Refurbishment-based noisy-label learning mixes an observed label with a model-derived pseudo target, typically using one sample-wise cleanliness score to control both branches. This creates a hidden coupling: reducing trust in the observed label automatically increases trust in the pseudo target. We show that this complementarity can replace one unreliable signal with another because a pseudo target learned from corrupted supervision may reproduce the noise it is meant to correct. Our representation diagnostics provide a consistent account of this mismatch: noisy supervision redirects deeper layers more strongly, whereas shallower relations remain comparatively stable and provide information beyond the loss posterior. We therefore propose TRACE, a Two-Source Reliability Assessment framework for Label Correction and Sample Reweighting. TRACE assesses the observed label using loss fit, shallow relation stability, and prediction agreement, while separately assessing the pseudo target using model confidence. Its source-specific scores control target correction and supervision strength without assuming complementary reliability. Across synthetic and real-world noisy benchmarks, TRACE improves representative refurbishment baselines and yields more reliable pseudo supervision.

</details>


### [260] [Enhancing Tabular Learners with Context-Aware Semantic Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03565)
*Günther Schindler,Maximilian Schambach,Johannes Höhne*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出CASE框架，利用LLM语义理解与表格学习器统计能力结合，通过预填充KV缓存实现上下文感知的行嵌入，在多个基准上显著提升低数据场景下的表格学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代表格学习器忽略特征名和单元格的语义信息，在语义真空下处理文本特征，导致无法充分利用数据中的丰富语义，尤其在数据稀缺时性能受限。

Method: CASE框架基于Gemma 3定制表格语言模型，通过预填充代表性行样本的KV缓存来建立数据集语义锚点，使生成的行嵌入动态上下文化，从而解决语义歧义并锚定领域特定上下文。

Result: 在CARTE、TextTab和TabArena基准上，CASE显著提升了表格学习器在语义丰富数据集上的性能，尤其在低数据 regime 下改进明显。

Conclusion: CASE有效弥合了LLM语义理解与表格统计学习之间的鸿沟，为语义丰富的表格任务提供了增强嵌入方法，确立了上下文感知在表格表示学习中的价值。

Abstract: While modern tabular learners excel at capturing statistical patterns, they frequently operate in a semantic vacuum, treating textual features as discrete symbols, ignoring the rich semantics inherent in feature names or cell entries. We propose CASE (Context-Aware Semantic Embeddings), a novel framework that bridges the gap between the semantic understanding of Large Language Models (LLMs) and the statistical capabilities of tabular learners. Unlike existing methods that embed rows in isolation, CASE utilizes a contextualization strategy: we pre-fill the KV cache of a custom-trained Gemma 3-based Tabular Language Model with a representative sample of rows to establish a persistent anchor of the dataset's semantics. This ensures that generated row embeddings are dynamically contextualized, resolving semantic ambiguities and anchoring representations in domain-specific context. Our experiments across several benchmarks (CARTE, TextTab, and TabArena) demonstrate that CASE substantially improves the performance of tabular learners on semantically rich datasets, particularly in low-data regimes.

</details>


### [261] [A Theory of Conditional Collapse under Low-Rank Weight-Space Ablations: I. The Single-Block Theory and Synthetic Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03620)
*Abdallah Khemais*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文研究了激活修补与权重空间消融在组件因果归因上的一致条件，通过理想化残差流模型推导了三个精确结果，并在合成任务与真实模型中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活修补和权重消融都声称组件对行为有因果作用，但分别作用于单次前向传播和参数本身，二者何时一致尚不明确。

Method: 建立了残差流加法模型F(x)=F0(x)+∑αi(x)vi，线性读出；严格证明了删除载体的对称性与外部对比条件，推导了修补与消融的读出差异公式，并给出了注意力头与层归一化、MLP组合的一阶交互公式及二阶余项。

Result: 三十九种消融配置下，理想模型预测准确率与实测交互的Spearman相关系数为-0.83；合成任务与架构复现了相同模式，包括极性反转；单块交互结果可推广至多个残差块。

Conclusion: 修补与消融在单载体层面互不约束，但整体交互模式可用理想化模型预测；理论在真实预训练模型中得到进一步验证。

Abstract: Activation patching and weight-space ablation both claim a component is causally responsible for a behavior, yet they act on different objects: one forward pass versus the parameters behind every forward pass. We ask when they agree.
  We study an idealized model where a conditional computation is carried additively through a residual stream, $F(x)=F_0(x)+\sum_iα_i(x)v_i$, read out by a linear functional, and prove three exact results. First, deleting a subset of carriers collapses a matched input pair onto the same unconditional output \emph{if and only if} the removal is symmetric on the pair and leaves no outside contrast; the error is deterministic, and we give its exact form even when the two conditions hold only approximately. Second, patching a carrier moves the readout by its donor-receiver \emph{contrast}, while ablating it moves the readout by its \emph{absolute level}; neither bounds the other, and we construct pairs where every single-carrier patch flips the decision while no single-carrier ablation does. Third, for an attention head composed with its own layer's normalization and MLP, we derive an exact first-order interaction formula with a provably second-order remainder, vanishing identically when only the MLP is ablated but not, in general, when a head is.
  Small transformers trained on a synthetic conditional task illustrate all three predictions: across thirty-nine ablation configurations the measured interaction is strongly rank-correlated with the idealized model's predictive accuracy (Spearman $-0.83$), and a second task and architecture reproduces the same pattern, including a further polarity reversal.
  The single-block interaction result extends past one residual block, and the synthetic validation is tested against a real pretrained model, in a companion paper that takes this theory further along both axes.

</details>


### [262] [POEM: Phase-Aware $\mathrm{SO}(2)$ Feature Rotation for Time Series Forecasting Under Periodicity Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03630)
*Jiawen Zhu,Shuhan Liu,Shengxuan Li,Qiming Shi,Di Weng*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出POEM框架，利用SO(2)旋转和DPIA注意力机制处理时间序列中的周期性漂移问题，提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在固定时间网格上建模，难以适应周期时序中相位变化导致的漂移问题。

Method: 学习相位校正坐标，对潜在特征施加可逆SO(2)旋转以降低相位变化；DPIA从相似历史上下文检索相位增量并外推至未来。

Result: 实验取得竞争性性能，可视化显示潜在轨迹更规则。

Conclusion: POEM有效缓解周期漂移，为相位感知时间序列预测提供新思路。

Abstract: Deep learning has advanced time series forecasting, but periodicity drift, in which cycle timing and phase vary over time, remains a challenging problem. Existing methods predominantly model these sequences on fixed time grids, suffering from a limited ability to accommodate phase-related variation. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{POEM}, a phase-aware forecasting framework based on latent feature rotation using the special orthogonal group in two dimensions, denoted by $\mathrm{SO}(2)$. POEM aims to reduce the phase-related variability by learning a phase-correction coordinate and applying an invertible $\mathrm{SO}(2)$-based rotation to paired latent features. To extrapolate this correction coordinate, Directional Phase Increment Attention (DPIA) retrieves historical phase increments from similar temporal contexts and integrates them into future phase corrections. Experiments demonstrate that POEM achieves competitive performance, while qualitative visualizations suggest that the learned phase-aware transformation makes latent trajectories more regular.

</details>


### [263] [Rethinking Modality Reliability in Multimodal Sentiment Analysis with Incomplete Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03611)
*Chunlei Meng,Jacqueline J. Pang,Pengbin Feng,Zhenyu Yu,Chun Ouyang,Zhongxue Gan*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出MRCF框架，显式建模模态可靠性以解决多模态情感分析中观测不完整时的可靠性错配和传播偏差问题，在三个基准数据集上取得强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不完整观测MSA方法（重建或联合表示）未显式建模模态可靠性，导致两个问题：可靠性错配（各模态情感证据随样本和缺失率变化）和可靠性传播偏差（退化模态消息干扰跨模态交互和预测）。

Method: MRCF包含三个组件：可靠性感知分支（从模态内质量线索和跨模态语义一致性估计样本级可靠性）、可靠性引导交互分支（用估计分数调节跨模态信息流）、可靠性校准融合模块（整合可靠性和语义线索进行预测）。

Result: 在CMU-MOSI、CMU-MOSEI和CH-SIMS上，MRCF在标准不完整观测协议下取得强性能；进一步分析证明显式可靠性建模有助于缓解可靠性错配和传播偏差。

Conclusion: 显式建模模态可靠性是处理不完整观测多模态情感分析的关键，MRCF通过校准可靠性有效提升鲁棒性和预测性能。

Abstract: Multimodal Sentiment Analysis (MSA) integrates text, audio, and vision to infer human affect, yet real-world multimodal observations are often incomplete. Existing methods for incomplete-observation MSA mainly follow two paradigms. Reconstruction-based methods recover missing information from observed modalities, while joint-representation methods learn directly from incomplete inputs. Although effective, these methods usually treat modality reliability only implicitly within representation learning or fusion design rather than modeling it explicitly. We argue that modality reliability is a central variable in incomplete-observation settings. Failure to model it explicitly gives rise to two related issues. The first is reliability mismatch, in which the affective evidence retained by each modality varies across samples and missing rates. The second is reliability propagation bias, in which messages from degraded modalities may adversely affect cross-modal interaction and predictive performance. To address these issues, we propose MRCF, a Modality Reliability-Calibrated Framework for MSA with incomplete observations. MRCF contains a Reliability-Aware Branch that estimates sample-specific modality reliability from intramodal quality cues and cross-modal semantic consistency, a Reliability-Guided Interaction Branch that uses the estimated scores to modulate cross-modal information flow, and a Reliability-Calibrated Fusion Module that integrates reliability and semantic cues for final prediction. Experiments on CMU-MOSI, CMU-MOSEI, and CH-SIMS show that MRCF achieves strong performance under standard incomplete-observation protocols. Further analyses provide evidence that explicit reliability modeling helps mitigate reliability mismatch and reliability propagation bias during interaction and fusion.

</details>


### [264] [Cross-Layer Interaction under Weight-Space Ablation: A Closed-Form Attention Jacobian Bound and a Test on a Real Pretrained Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03629)
*Abdallah Khemais*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文扩展了先前关于激活修补与权重消融一致性的理论结果，将单层单块模型推广到多层，并精确分离跨层交互余项，给出了注意力子块雅可比界的闭式解，同时在真实模型上发现并测试了间接宾语识别涌现电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作仅局限于单个残差块和小型合成任务，其交互公式无法直接应用于多层真实模型，且未给出曲率常数的显式形式，需要更通用的理论框架和实际验证。

Method: 通过将跨层消融交互分解为逐层同块项与跨层余项，用二重积分精确表示两层余项，推导注意力子块雅可比界和曲率常数的闭式解，并在Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct真实权重上验证，同时用激活修补搜索涌现电路并测试三种属性。

Result: 雅可比界在真实权重上无一违反；涌现电路在五个实例中均有共享载体，但坍缩和分离仅在大多数（非全部）实例成立，五个中有三个在非同类层对上存在可测交互；曲率常数已给出闭式形式。

Conclusion: 理论框架成功扩展到多层并锁定关键未解环节（跨层链式雅可比界），但涌现电路的预测属性并不完全稳定，说明真实模型中的交互复杂性和现有理论的边界仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: A companion paper studies when activation patching and weight-space ablation agree, inside an idealized model where a conditional computation is carried additively through a residual stream. For the one composition in that model where two carriers are architecturally dependent, an attention head and its own layer's normalization-MLP composition, it derives an exact first-order interaction formula, zero when only the MLP is ablated and second-order bounded when the head is also ablated. That result is confined to a single residual block and checked only on small transformers on a synthetic task.
  This paper extends the result past both limits. First, the interaction from ablating carriers spanning several layers decomposes exactly into same-block terms, one per touched layer, plus a cross-layer remainder on which the decomposition makes no claim of smallness. Second, we isolate that remainder exactly, for two layers, as a double integral of a mixed second derivative, and name the missing ingredient needed to bound it: a Jacobian bound for the attention sub-block. We derive this bound in closed form and verify it, without a single violation, against Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct's real weights, though we do not yet chain it across layers. We also give, in closed form, the curvature constant the companion paper's bound leaves unexhibited.
  Third, on that same model, we search for and find an emergent circuit for indirect object identification, never designed into it, using the original activation-patching method for this task, and test collapse, dissociation, and interaction on it. The result is mixed: a shared carrier emerges across all five tested instances, collapse and dissociation hold on most but not all, and a nonzero interaction is measurable on three of five, at layer pairs outside the same-block case the companion theorem covers.

</details>


### [265] [LAEF: A Lead-Agnostic ECG Foundation Model Towards Point-of-Care Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03690)
*Edoardo Coppola,Stefano Fiorini,Pietro Liò,Mattia Savardi,Alberto Signoroni*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出LAEF，一种7M参数的导联无关心电图基础模型，通过将ECG建模为可变大小时空图并采用图注意力网络，原生支持任意导联子集，在1-2导联点护设备场景下优于零填充方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ECG基础模型通常固定为12导联输入，难以适应智能手表等点护设备常见的1-2导联采集，导致性能下降或失效。

Method: 将ECG表示为具有生理驱动的导联内和导联间连接的变大小时空图，用图注意力网络处理；通过9.2M条12导联ECG上的掩码节点建模和随机导联采样预训练，实现导联无关表示。

Result: 在18个下游数据集中，全导联下与12倍规模的12导联专用基线相当；1-2导联下在17/18（单导联）和14/18（双导联）数据集上超过零填充方法，平均AUROC提升+3.2点；跨164种心血管疾病的导联重要性研究表明单导联下性能稳定。

Conclusion: 架构级导联无关性是性能优势的关键，LAEF有效支持点护场景的少导联ECG分析，同时保持临床导联-疾病关联的可解释性。

Abstract: Point-of-care cardiac devices such as smartwatches and handheld ECG recorders typically capture 1--2 leads, yet existing ECG foundation models are architecturally constrained to fixed 12-lead inputs, degrading or failing under these reduced configurations. We introduce LAEF (Lead-Agnostic ECG Foundation), a 7M-parameter ECG foundation model that can natively process any lead subset without zero-padding or architectural modification. LAEF represents ECGs as variable-size spatiotemporal graphs with physiologically motivated intra- and inter-lead connectivity, processed by a Graph Attention Network that scales naturally with active lead count.Pre-trained on 9.2M 12-lead ECGs via masked node modelling with stochastic lead sampling, LAEF learns representations robust to lead configuration. Across 18 downstream datasets, LAEF is on par with specialized 12-lead baselines over 12$\times$ larger at full lead availability. Under direct point-of-care-oriented diagnostics (1--2 leads), it outperforms all zero-padded alternatives on 17 out of 18 datasets with with a single randomly sampled lead and on 14 out of 18 with 2 leads, with an average AUROC gain of +3.2 points. Representation analysis links this advantage to architectural lead-agnosticism, and a lead-importance study across 164 cardiovascular conditions shows population-level performance is stable across single standard input leads while still recovering established clinically lead-condition associations.

</details>


### [266] [Learning and Clustering on Temporal Graphs: Principles, Primitives, and Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03696)
*Nelson Aloysio Reis de Almeida Passos,Emanuele Carlini,Salvatore Trani*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文系统探讨了时序图上的聚类问题，从原理、原语和池化三个视角对比了算法方法与图神经网络，指出在无属性或弱属性场景下算法方法更合适，并提出了GPU加速的时序聚类原语作为理论支撑的池化算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管图神经网络在许多图任务上表现优异，但在聚类任务中，其相比传统描述性和推断性聚类算法的优势尚不明确，尤其是在效率和恢复精度方面。本文旨在通过三个关联视角厘清这一张力，并探索理论驱动的池化操作。

Method: 从谱理论和随机块模型的可检测性阈值出发，建立图学习与社区检测的共同原理；实现基于GPU加速的谱聚类和多切片模块度优化作为时序原语；将社区检测视为一种有理论依据的粗粒化池化算子。

Result: 在无属性或弱属性数据上，算法方法在可扩展性上优于神经网络；而当结构、时序和属性信号一致时，神经模型更具优势。GPU加速的原语使时序聚类可扩展，并支持理论驱动的池化。

Conclusion: 算法方法仍是弱属性场景下的首选工具，而神经模型在多重信号对齐时更有效；社区检测作为池化算子的可行性依赖于其能否保持下游任务所需的动态信息，这是一个亟待回答的核心问题。

Abstract: This work focuses on the problem of learning on temporal graphs, with particular emphasis on the task of clustering: obtaining coarse-grained representations by aggregating information from nodes, edges, and temporal dynamics - a task related to pooling in machine learning on graphs, or community detection in network science. Although graph neural networks reach state-of-the-art performance across many downstream graph tasks, their advantage over established descriptive and inferential clustering algorithms is far less settled, especially under demands of efficiency and recovery accuracy. We frame this tension through three linked perspectives: principles, connecting graph learning and community detection through shared spectral foundations and detectability thresholds in stochastic block model regimes; primitives, making spectral clustering and multislice modularity optimization tractable through GPU-accelerated temporal backends; and pooling, viewing principled community detection as a theory-grounded coarse-graining operator for temporal graphs. Our results indicate that algorithmic methods remain the appropriate tool where attributes are absent or weak - scalability rather than accuracy being the binding obstacle - while neural models are most compelling when structural, temporal, and attribute signals align. By making temporal clustering scalable, GPU-accelerated primitives suggest a route toward theory-grounded pooling, while raising a central question: when does community-based coarse-graining preserve the dynamics needed for downstream learning tasks?

</details>


### [267] [To Describe or Construct Statistical Learning Models Using the Category-theoretical Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03706)
*Congwei Song*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文以范畴论视角概述经典统计学习模型与算法，旨在吸引包括基础数学在内的其他领域研究者参与统计学习研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计学习虽是机器学习和人工智能的主流，但现有资料对初学者不够友好，且跨领域研究者参与不足，因此需要一份系统综述并提供新的理论视角以促进交叉研究。

Method: 简述了若干经典统计学习模型和知名算法，并从范畴论的角度重新诠释这些模型，以建立统一的形式化理解框架。

Result: 总结了经典模型与算法，并展示了范畴论在统计学习中的潜在解释力，为跨学科读者提供了入门指引和理论启发。

Conclusion: 通过范畴论视角和通俗综述，可降低统计学习的入门门槛，促进基础数学等领域的学者加入相关研究。

Abstract: Statistical learning is a fascinating field that has long been the mainstream of machine learning/artificial intelligence. A large number of results have been produced which can be widely applied to real-world problems. It also leads to many research topics and also stimulates new research. This report summarizes some classical statistical learning models and well-known algorithms, especially for amateurs, and provides a category-theoretic perspective on understanding statistical learning models. The aim is to attract researchers from other fields, including basic mathematics, to participate in the research related to statistical learning.

</details>


### [268] [CRS-Triage: Confidence- and Reliability-Aware Selective Triage under Incomplete Clinical Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03862)
*Guan Qiang,Yushen Chen,Tianlong Liu,David Rotenberg,Ethan H. Kim,Fang Fang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出CRS-Triage，一种置信度与可靠性感知的选择性分诊预测方法，通过分别评估结构化数据和临床文本的可靠性并联合考虑模态一致性来估计预测置信度，可选择性决定模型决策或延迟处理，并通过对分诊不足错误施加惩罚来降低高危患者漏诊风险。在MIMIC-IV-ED上验证了有效性、风险-覆盖率权衡和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊分诊需在短时间内做出可靠决策，但EHR数据（结构化数据与临床文本）常不完整、不可靠且不一致，现有ML模型依赖完整可靠数据，难以准确预测患者病情严重程度，因此需要一种能够处理数据质量问题的分诊预测方法。

Method: CRS-Triage分别评估结构化数据和临床文本的可靠性，然后联合考虑两种模态间的一致性，估计每个预测的置信度；通过将置信度分数与预定义阈值比较，选择性决定模型是作出决策还是延迟该病例；同时，通过惩罚分诊不足错误，模型倾向于将患者分配到略高严重程度，降低高危患者漏诊风险。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV-ED数据集上的实验表明，CRS-Triage取得了强的预测性能，提供了更好的风险-覆盖率权衡，并且在EHR数据不完整、退化或跨模态不一致时仍保持可靠。

Conclusion: CRS-Triage通过置信度和可靠性感知的选择性机制，有效应对了不完整、不可靠和不一致的EHR数据，提高了分诊预测的可靠性和安全性，尤其减少了对高危患者的分诊不足风险。

Abstract: Emergency triage requires reliable decisions within a short time period. However, the available electronic health record (EHR) data, including structured data and clinical text, are often incomplete, unreliable, and inconsistent. This makes machine learning (ML)-based triage prediction more challenging, as existing ML models typically rely on complete and reliable EHR data to accurately predict patients' acuity levels. To address this, we propose confidence- and reliability-aware selective triage (CRS-Triage) to predict patients' acuity levels with a confidence score. By comparing the confidence score with a predefined threshold, CRS-Triage can selectively determine whether the model should make the decision or defer the case. Specifically, CRS-Triage separately evaluates the reliability of structured data and clinical text and then jointly considers the consistency between the two modalities to estimate the confidence of each prediction. Moreover, to reduce the risk of missing high-acuity patients, namely under-triage, CRS-Triage prefers to assign patients slightly higher acuity levels, namely over-triage, by penalizing under-triage errors. Experiments on the MIMIC-IV-ED dataset show that CRS-Triage achieves strong predictive performance. It also provides a better risk-coverage trade-off and remains reliable when the available EHR data are incomplete, degraded, or inconsistent across modalities.

</details>


### [269] [AgenticECO: An Agentic Framework for ECO on 3D Integrated Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03738)
*Shuo Ren,Yaohui Han,Libo Shen,Zhiqiang Jia,Rongliang Fu,Bei Yu,Tsung-Yi Ho*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出了AgenticECO与EcoRoute，在3D-IC ECO流程中实现可归因的、最小扰动修复，显著优于全重布线基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摩尔定律放缓推动3D集成，但3D-IC中路由后ECO依赖手工且修复与无关重布线混叠，导致无法将结果归因于具体编辑。

Method: AgenticECO是证据门控的工具使用代理工作流，EcoRoute是最小扰动ECO路由层，驱动未修改的固定路由器以实现编辑级可归因性。

Result: 九个缺陷中清除七个（对比基线两个），平均扰动0.66%，零时钟网触碰；跨骨干重跑清除全部九个。对照实验验证必要性、合法着陆与时钟裕度影响；预注册研究精确恢复缺陷且零错误编辑。

Conclusion: AgenticECO与EcoRoute在3D-IC ECO中提供可归因、低扰动且可靠的自动修复，全部结果通过完整物理验证门禁。

Abstract: As Moore's law slows, the industry is turning to three-dimensional integration; yet in merged 3D-IC flows, routed designs expose bond-level defects with no 2D analogue, and post-route engineering change orders (ECO) remain manual, expertise-bound work. Worse, the standard edit-then-fully-reroute practice entangles a repair with router churn, so a signoff number cannot be attributed to the edit that motivated it. We present AgenticECO, an evidence-gated tool-using agent workflow for 3D-IC ECO on the open-source TaiWei flow, paired with EcoRoute, a minimal-disturbance ECO-routing layer that drives the unmodified pinned router so a repair is attributable to its edit. Across nine matched natural defect cases under identical budgets, AgenticECO clears seven versus two for both full reroute and stock repair, at 0.66\% mean disturbance over cleared cases and zero clock nets touched, and a cross-backbone rerun under the same sealed contract clears all nine. Controlled studies show that the repair moves are necessary under preservation, that occupancy-aware choice buys legal landings rather than repair success, and that under tightened clocks minimal disturbance flips accept versus reject. Three preregistered visual studies localize the pixel instrument's edge to contested landing sites, and a preregistered blind diagnostic exactly restores every held-out injected defect, the only arm with zero wrong edits. Every accepted result passes routing, fresh extraction, max/min timing, DRC, and structural-equivalence gates. Code, environment, and per-episode audit artifacts are released as supplementary material.

</details>


### [270] [Computing Actual Causes for Neural Network Predictions under Structured Causal Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03772)
*Jannick Strobel,Muqsit Azeem,Stefan Leue*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出用Halpern-Pearl实际因果形式化神经网络预测解释，通过布尔结构因果模型建模输入依赖，结合界传播与分支定界算法高效计算所有最小实际原因，实验证明其可扩展性显著优于基线，并揭示忽略依赖会致14.9%虚假解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解释方法（如特征归因或最小充分集）常假设输入特征独立，在输入存在结构化依赖时会产生误导性解释，缺乏因果层面的严谨形式化。

Method: 将解释定义为HP实际原因，用布尔SCM建模输入依赖；采用界传播与分支定界搜索计算完备且最小的原因集合，提供完整性与最小性保证。

Result: 在搜索空间达2.3×10^13候选对、SCM节点数至28的实例上，180秒预算内计算全部最小实际原因；规模上大幅超越暴力搜索与ILP基线，且随图增长优于启发式搜索；案例显示忽略依赖会多报14.9%虚假原因。

Conclusion: 基于SCM与HP因果的解释方法在考虑输入依赖时更可靠，且具备实际可扩展性，为可信AI解释提供了因果严谨且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Explaining the predictions of neural networks is a central challenge in trustworthy AI. Existing explanation methods, such as those based on feature attribution or minimal sufficient sets, typically treat input features as independent, which can yield misleading explanations when inputs exhibit structured dependencies. We address this by formalizing explanations as Halpern-Pearl (HP) actual causes, modeling input dependencies using Boolean Structural Causal Models (SCMs). We compute HP causes by applying bound propagation and branch-and-bound techniques, while providing formal guarantees of completeness and minimality. Our experiments show that we substantially outperform brute-force and ILP baselines in scalability, and outperform heuristic search as graph size grows, computing all minimal actual causes on instances with search spaces of up to $2.3\times10^{13}$ candidate (cause, contingency) pairs, on SCMs with up to 28 nodes, within a 180s per-instance budget. In a case study, we further show that ignoring input dependencies inflates the number of reported causes, 14.9% of which are spurious under our SCM.

</details>


### [271] [AgentStream: How Well Do Self-Evolving LLM Agents Perform Under Streaming Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00155)
*Dong Yan,Jian Liang,Dapeng Hu,Ran He,Nicholas Jing Yuan,Qi Zhang,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出AgentStream框架，在可配置任务流中评估自进化智能体，发现自进化可靠性随流场景变化、收益受模型能力门控且非单调，无单一方法全面占优，倡导在真实任务流中评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化智能体研究多采用独立评估，忽略了真实流式场景中任务多样性与复杂性的影响，导致其行为理解不足。

Method: 构建AgentStream统一框架，将多种智能体基准组织为可配置任务流，实例化隔离、顺序、交错三种流式场景，组合评估五种自进化方法在三个前沿基础模型上的表现。

Result: 自进化可靠性随流场景变化；收益受模型能力门控且与模型强度呈非单调关系；没有方法在所有模型和场景中占优。

Conclusion: 自进化智能体应在真实任务流而非孤立单任务设置中评估，结果为跨模型与场景的方法选择提供了具体指导。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can self-evolve by continually improving from their own accumulated experience. However, existing studies predominantly adopt independent evaluation. Consequently, the behavior of self-evolving agents in realistic streaming settings, where agents adapt to diverse and complex task streams, remains poorly understood. To address this gap, we introduce AgentStream, a unified framework that evaluates self-evolving agents spanning diverse evolution components by organizing agentic benchmarks into a configurable task stream and instantiating the \texttt{Isolated}, \texttt{Sequential}, and \texttt{Interleaved} streaming scenarios at test time, which progressively vary the scope and domain composition of the stream. Over these scenarios, we combinatorially evaluate five representative self-evolving methods across three frontier foundation models, disentangling how model capability, method architecture, and streaming scenario jointly shape self-evolution. Our results show that self-evolution reliability varies across streaming scenarios, the benefit of self-evolution is gated by model capability and non-monotonic in model strength, and no single method dominates across models and scenarios. These findings offer concrete guidance for selecting self-evolving methods across models and streaming scenarios. Overall, we advocate that self-evolving agents should be evaluated under realistic task streams rather than isolated single-task settings.

</details>


### [272] [Socially Grounded Agentic AI: Coordinating Plural Perspectives through Social Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03910)
*Matt Ratto,Abhishek Moturu,Daniel Silver*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出将多元对齐重新定位为社会性协调问题，而非简单的输出多样化，并基于社会学理论为AI系统设计提供具体方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐常假设单一价值体系，但实际社会情境中存在多种合法视角。当前多元对齐方法缺乏对价值如何在社会中被组织、竞争和协调的理论解释，因此需要引入社会学视角。

Method: 借鉴社会学传统，分析视角如何由角色结构化、通过互动塑造并在权力与专业领域间分布，进而转化为具体设计启示，包括基于角色的表征、视角间的结构化协调和情境敏感评估。对于智能体系统，强调需对齐角色激活、协商轨迹、聚合规则和反馈循环等过程。

Result: 提出了一个设计空间，使系统能以结构化且可问责的方式纳入多元视角，并指出了未来在真实环境中实施和实证评估的方向。

Conclusion: 多元对齐应被视为社会性协调问题，而非仅增加输出多样性；社会学理论为构建更负责任的多视角AI系统提供了关键资源和设计路径。

Abstract: As AI systems are deployed across increasingly diverse social contexts, alignment can no longer be framed as the optimization of a single, unified set of values. Instead, systems must be able to recognize, represent, and respond to multiple legitimate perspectives. This has led to growing interest in pluralistic alignment, which seeks to move beyond one-size-fits-all models of appropriate behaviour. However, current approaches often lack a clear account of how values are socially organized, contested, and coordinated in practice. In this paper, we argue that social theory provides essential conceptual and design resources for addressing these challenges. Drawing on established traditions in sociology, we show how perspectives can be understood as structured by roles, shaped through interaction, and distributed across fields of power and expertise. We translate these insights into concrete implications for AI system design, including role-based representations, structured coordination among perspectives, and context-sensitive evaluation. For agentic systems, this requires aligning not only final outputs, but also the role activations, deliberative traces, aggregation rules, and feedback loops through which those outputs are produced. Our contribution is to reposition pluralistic alignment as a problem of socially grounded coordination rather than output diversification. We outline a design space for systems that engage multiple perspectives in structured and accountable ways, and we identify directions for future work to implement and empirically evaluate these approaches in real-world settings.

</details>


### [273] [Implementing Causal Perception: Competing SCMs and Situated Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03917)
*Jose M. Álvarez*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文首次实现了因果感知框架，通过算法计算干预和反事实分布，并基于德国信用数据集展示了因果感知对多专家决策中准确性和公平性的影响，发现公平性判断对距离度量和阈值敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果感知虽有理论框架但缺乏实际实现，且其如何影响公平性评估和决策尚未被实证验证，需要操作性定义和算法支撑。

Method: 将因果感知分为结构性（图不同）和参数性（权重不同）两类，设计算法计算干预/反事实分布，采用距离度量量化分歧，并在德国信用数据集上模拟多专家决策场景。

Result: 因果感知会改变公平性评估和基于阈值的决策；感知结论对距离度量选择和阈值敏感；公平性偏见取决于主体的SCM，不同世界观无法忽视。

Conclusion: 因果感知的复杂性表明公平性具有情境依赖性，竞争性SCM下的偏见不可忽略，需在决策框架中显式建模代理人观点的分歧。

Abstract: Causal perception occurs when agents with competing Structural Causal Models (SCMs) of the same system infer different probability distributions, including the hypothetical distributions implied by each agent's SCM under the same set of interventions. It shapes how agents reason about the system and how they perceive its fairness. Causal perception is a promising probabilistic framework, but it has remained purely theoretical. This work provides the first implementation of the causal perception framework of Álvarez and Ruggieri (2025). We operationalize structural (agents disagree on the causal graph) and parametrical (agents agree on the causal graph but disagree on its weights) causal perception. We design algorithms for computing interventional and counterfactual distributions and propose suitable distance measures to quantify the disagreement. Using the German Credit dataset, we illustrate how causal perception affects accuracy and fairness in a multi-expert decision setting. We show that the perception verdict is sensitive to the choice of distance metric and threshold. We also show that causal perception changes fairness assessments and threshold-based decisions. Bias proves situated with respect to the agent's SCM, demonstrating that competing worldviews in fairness problems cannot be ignored.

</details>


### [274] [Should We Type or Talk to LLM Agents? A Comprehensive Study of Voice and Keyboard Input Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03970)
*Zizhao Hu,Nathan Elijah Segura,Mohammad Rostami,Jesse Thomason*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出HIVE框架，系统比较语音转写扰动与键盘输入扰动对LLM性能的影响，发现语音扰动因破坏原词而显著降低准确率，键盘扰动影响较小，且思维预算能恢复键盘性能但无法改善语音。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过打字或语音向语言模型输入信息，不同输入通道会引入特定噪声（如键盘的拼写错误、语音的转写不流畅），但目前缺乏对这些扰动如何影响LLM性能的系统研究。

Method: 构建HIVE（Human Input-Variation Engine），包含QWERTY键盘扰动和语音转写扰动，在多种指令微调模型上评测其鲁棒性，并分析扰动机制（如标记存活率、任务类型、数据污染、轻量适配和思维预算）。

Result: 七项发现：语音转写扰动普遍降低准确率，且主要源于转写结构而非填充词；键盘扰动代价较小；两者共同机制是破坏原词而非添加新词；通道差异仅在需要构造或推导答案的任务中出现；伤害不源于测试集污染；轻量适配无法消除；思维预算可恢复键盘性能但对语音无效，且压缩语音在思维预算下更差。

Conclusion: LLM对不同输入通道的扰动呈现不对称鲁棒性，语音扰动更难应对，提示未来需在模型训练或输入处理中针对性解决语音转写带来的结构破坏问题。

Abstract: Human input reaches language models by typing or speaking, and each channel leaves a distinct signature: orthographic noise for keyboards; for voice, disfluency from conventional transcription and restructuring from AI-backed dictation tools. How do they impact an LLM's performance? In this paper we present HIVE (Human Input-Variation Engine), a suite of voice transcription perturbations and QWERTY keyboard perturbations. We use HIVE to evaluate how robust models are to these perturbations. We present seven findings. (i) Voice transcription perturbations lower accuracy across every instruction-tuned model we test, and it is the structure of the transcription rather than its fillers that carries the cost. (ii) QWERTY keyboard perturbations cost less, and a model absorbs a lot of them before accuracy falls away. (iii) Both trace back to one cause, how many of the question's tokens survive the perturbation: destroying a token is what hurts, while adding new ones alongside it costs little. (iv) The gap between the two channels appears only where the answer must be constructed or deduced; on multiple choice there is none. (v) The harm does not solely come from test-set contamination. (vi) It cannot be trained away with lightweight adaptation. (vii) A thinking budget recovers the keyboard channel almost entirely but leaves the spoken registers untouched, and compressed speech is worse with it.

</details>
